Only INFERENCE still pairs cameras with `communication/communicator.py::_pick_synced_camera_msgs` (15 ms slop, 8-deep rings; owner decision C3: unchanged, its walk-back can hand the policy a pair up to ~233 ms old, `docs/KNOWN-ISSUES.md`); the recorder's frames are decided by the slot sampler (next bullet).
- **Every dataset frame is a decided slot** (round 5, owner decision R5-1; `communication/capture_timeline.py`, stdlib only). Before, the recorder sampled whatever was cached when its tick ran: aliasing (a repeat, then a skip), the pair picker's walk-back, and a late tick losing its slot — 350–451 of 600 frames kept per 10 s take, 10–24 % repeats, a timeline 2.8–4.3 s off. Now every registered source has a PRE-CREATED `SourceHistory` ring (`Communicator.init_subscribers`; camera `CAMERA_HISTORY_LEN` 64, follower/leader `JOINT_HISTORY_LEN` 256 — never created lazily inside a callback, which raced the recorder's snapshot) of `(seq, arrival, t, msg)`: each callback takes `time.monotonic()` first, appends, then does its old work; `clear_latest_data()` clears the rings (seq keeps counting). `t` is the header stamp mapped onto the monotonic clock by `ClockMap` (running minimum of arrival − stamp over 2 s, clamped to [arrival − 0.5 s, arrival], non-decreasing; stamp 0 → arrival), which absorbs the three clock domains (bridge `capture_ns`, V4L2, ros2_control) and places a late callback where it happened. `SlotSampler(fps, cameras).decide_due(now, *communicator.history_snapshots())` is the only entry point, called every tick BEFORE the recorder lock (pure, < 1 ms): slot k is `t0 + k/fps` (`t0` = the session's first tick; the grid runs through warm-up, takes and resets); a slot is decided once, when PROVABLY complete (every camera has a sample beyond its boundary, follower and leader beyond the observation instant; streams arrive in order) or `MARGIN_S` (0.1 s) after its earliest decision time (a silent camera then repeats, counted); at most `MAX_SLOTS_PER_TICK` (2) per call; slots more than `CATCHUP_MAX_S` (0.25 s) overdue are given up (lost, counted), so a late tick never turns into an encoder-starving burst. The resulting stall bound (measured on the full build, round 7): a process stall up to ~0.30 s loses nothing; 320 ms lost one slot in 7 of 50 takes, 340–500 ms in 70–85 %; every loss is counted and warned (C5), and the action age reaches 113 ms at a 350 ms stall. Per camera the choice is the newest frame at or before `G + φ + h`: `φ` tracks the camera's phase against the grid with a second-order predictor (the measured rate predicts the per-slot drift: a camera 1–1.5 % off the fps fell from up to 11 % excess repeat+skip to ≤ 0.07 %) and hysteresis (one honest repeat or skip per drift cycle); a camera is locked within `LOCK_TOL` 0.06 × m of an integer multiple m of the fps (a 26 Hz camera at 25 fps: excess 7.75 % → 0.23 %), provisionally while its rate is unknown; the rate comes from the sampler's own per-camera series bounded by the slot time (a slot decided late after a stall is decided exactly as on time); a chosen frame is never older than the camera's last chosen one. The observation instant `R` is the newest chosen image's `t`; state and action are the newest samples at or before `R`, the unstamped leader back-dated one median period per message inside a delivery burst (action-age p99 under stalls 70 → 15 ms). Fresh frames only (F1, refined per C2-A): a frame is used again only when its camera delivered nothing newer by its own boundary; the cross-camera spread is the cameras' phase difference and is never bought down with a stale frame. **The camera-latency skew** (rig gate G3): an image's `t` is its stamp mapped to its earliest arrival, so its CONTENT is older than `R` by the camera's pipeline latency, while state and action are the newest at `R` — the convention LeRobot's `read_latest()` and inference share (mock: median 8–22 ms, SD 5–13 ms). During a take with a dataset the tick (`physical_ai_server.py::_record_decided_slots`) records, in order, exactly the decided slots at or after the take's run entry until the take ends or a trip is pending, decoding only the chosen camera messages (the latest frames are not decoded during a take); every other phase calls `record()` with the latest data once per tick, and the data gates, dataset creation and the source-stop rule keep using `get_latest_data()`. **A take ends by frame count**, never by wall clock: `n_target = max(1, round(episode_time_s · fps))`; a call without images adds nothing. The dataset `timestamp` stays LeRobot's `frame_index / fps` and is now true (measured on the full build, mock cameras, 2 × 10 s per case: 600/600 frames in every case, repeats and skips = the cameras' own honest rates, max timeline error 24–46 ms; `docs/CLAUDE-CHANGELOG.md`). **Per take, `TakeIntegrity`** (reset at each run entry) collects frames, per camera repeats/skips/rate and the repeats in excess of the rate, lost/late/timed-out slots, the oldest state/action at `R`, the spread p95, and per source the largest gap on the source's OWN timeline; one English line per take (`capture take ep=… {json}`), and its German C5 warning (`warning_de`: a lost slot, a camera's excess repeats ≥ 2 and > 2 % of the frames — beyond its rate, with its skips netted first (round 6: a camera at the full rate whose stamps jitter makes repeat/skip PAIRS, which once read „zu wenige Bilder") —, or state/action older than 0.1 s; F3: warn only, never a re-record) rides the tick that committed the take, only when no other warning is set. The gap verdict (`gap_source()`: ≥ `SOURCE_GAP_S`, an open gap at the take's end included; a leader gap counts only when the follower's DELIVERY shows no pause overlapping it — a server stall delays both, a real dropout does not) goes to `DataManager.note_take_gap` after every frame, and `save()` re-records the take for it (O2/C6; the caps are in Rule §2). Frame loss in the encoder is read from LeRobot's PUBLIC warning, never a private counter (owner decision Q2): `data_processing/encoder_drop_watch.py` is one `logging.Handler` on `lerobot.datasets.video_utils` parsing „Encoder queue full for <key>, dropped N frame(s)" (logged on the 1st drop of an episode, then every 10th) and the finish summary, re-armed at a take's first frame; detection is exact, the count a lower bound (hevc 120 = 120, h264 1 vs 5), so the German sentence names no number. After a save, `_verify_saved_lengths` compares each video span with `length / fps` (half a frame tolerance: LeRobot's train-time `FrameTimestampError` condition; detect + inform).
- **One recorder lock; the trip and the commands never wait for it** (round 5, owner decisions O1/O6 with F5/F9). `DataManager.lock` (an RLock; every public mutator runs under it) is taken with `locked()`, which at release applies a collision discard that arrived meanwhile and re-checks after releasing, so a request is never lost; `request_collision_discard()` NEVER blocks the trip (applied now when the lock is free, else by the holder at release). The record tick captures `_record_session_gen` at entry, returns at once while not recording or while `_collision_trip_pending` is set, and re-checks all three AFTER taking the lock (`_record_tick_owns_session`); `_publish_record_status` refuses under the same conditions (publish guard `_record_publish_lock`, held only around flag/generation changes, never across work). START bumps the generation and sets `start_recording_time` BEFORE the timer starts (in its own group the first tick could otherwise time out at once). **FINISH and STOP are never refused as busy** (round 6, owner decision F1): `DataManager.request_end('finish'|'stop')` never waits for the lock — applied at once when it is free, else queued and applied the moment the holder releases it (after a pending collision discard, which goes first; Q3/Q4 judged when applied) — so „Wiederholen" then FINISH during the official ~1 s discard always ends the session; the answer is success, with `FINISH_QUEUED_DE` on a record session only while a discard is really in flight (`DataManager.discard_in_flight()`, round 7: an end queued behind an ordinary record step — about half of all presses — gets the normal answer) (measured: 0 of 24 direct and 0 of 12 old-client FINISHes busy, 20 page runs each ending with exactly one episode). Every other record branch of `user_interaction_callback` takes the lock with `try_locked(COMMAND_LOCK_TIMEOUT_S)` (0.05 s since round 6, F2) and on timeout changes nothing and answers `record_texts_de.BUSY_DE` („Die Aufnahme ist gerade beschäftigt. Bitte versuch es gleich noch einmal."), so `/task/command` never holds the default group longer than that. `check_lerobot_dataset` does its slow I/O (the hub check, a download, the creation) without the lock and installs the dataset under it. `_save_count_pending` (the latched save really committed frames) is read at all four count sites, so an empty latched save never counts (I2j). Every German sentence of these paths is `data_processing/record_texts_de.py` (stdlib only, loaded by path where the deps-free loaders give the package no `__path__`); the two prefixes and `BUSY_DE` are byte-equal with the page (`robotis_ai_setup/tests/test_record_r5_lockstep.py`).
- **A dataset is never overwritten and never silently stretched** (round 5). D7: with upload on, `_check_dataset_exists` asks the Hub LOGGED IN (`HfApi()` with the rig's token: `whoami()` then `repo_exists()`), in a thread joined after `HUB_CHECK_TIMEOUT_S` (15 s); True → the sync download through the Daten download worker, then resume; False → create. A failure to ask is never „absent" — the anonymous request it replaced read a PRIVATE hub dataset as „absent" (401/404), and the end-of-session upload + orphan sweep then overwrote it. Round 6 (F4) splits the failures by cause: no token, or a token the hub refuses at `whoami()` → the session records WITHOUT upload (`push_to_hub` off for that session, nothing on the hub can be overwritten) and the next status tick's `[WARNUNG]` is `UPLOAD_OFF_NO_TOKEN_DE` / `UPLOAD_OFF_TOKEN_INVALID_DE` (prefix `UPLOAD_OFF_PREFIX_DE`); the repo query refused (401/403) → refused with `HUB_CHECK_AUTH_DE` (the token, „oder schalte … das Hochladen aus"); an UNREACHABLE hub (network, 429, 5xx, no answer in time) no longer refuses (Daten 2.0): the session records, its first status tick says `OFFLINE_START_DE`, `_sync_base` stays `SYNC_UNCHECKED`, and the guarded upload decides at the end (`HUB_CHECK_REFUSED_DE` is gone). D4: a resumed dataset must pass LeRobot's own `sanity_check_dataset_robot_compatibility` with the features the current frames would create (`data_manager.py::_resume_compatibility_check`); the first mismatching field picks the German refusal (`fps:` → „… mit {d} Bildern pro Sekunde aufgenommen …", `features:`, `robot_type:`); before, a resume at another fps silently stretched or compressed the episodes.
- **Dataset names and the disk floor.** The task part of a repo name is `data_manager.py::safe_dataset_task_name`, in this order: NFC → German pairs (ä→ae ö→oe ü→ue Ä→Ae Ö→Oe Ü→Ue ß→ss ẞ→SS) → NFKD accent fold (drop category-`M` marks: é→e, ç→c) → the old sanitiser `[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]` → `-` → collapse runs of `-` and of `.` → strip `-` (the old sanitiser emitted `--`/`..`, which the Hub refuses). The user-id part keeps its old rule (`safe_dataset_user_id`: sanitise, strip `-`, empty/dot-only → `unknown-user`; no transliteration, since the namespace guard compares it with whoami names). React's `utils/datasetName.js` is byte-identical (the `u` flag is mandatory, marks via `\p{M}`), and both sides read one shared fixture, `physical_ai_manager/src/utils/__tests__/datasetName.cases.json`, generated from the server functions. Consequence: a local dataset whose name had umlauts/accents/runs is not resumed under its old name. Disk floors (`signal_status.py`): START_RECORD below `DISK_START_FLOOR_BYTES` (3 GB) is refused before the `_mode_lock` claim with a German sentence — for every caller, the check does not look at `task_type`; an unreadable disk allows the start (refuse on proof only); below `DISK_CRITICAL_FLOOR_BYTES` (1 GB) the running session ends like FINISH (`finish_for_low_disk`, throttled 1 Hz, only in warm-up/run/reset). Why these numbers: critical 1 GB ≈ the save transient (a 200 MB video chunk re-muxed through `/tmp`, ~0.45 GB) + 120 s of worst-case video (3.6 MB/s for two cameras) + margin; start 3 GB = critical + ~33 min at the measured 1 MB/s planning rate. In WSL2 the container measures the dynamic `ext4.vhdx`, not C: (`docs/KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).
- **`/edubotics/signal_status` reports facts, never verdicts** (`physical_ai_server/signal_status.py`, schema v1, `std_msgs/String` JSON): per configured camera, the follower and (only when the profile `has_leader`) the leader — `hz` over ~2 s and `age_s` of the last message, `null` while not measurable — plus the dataset disk's free bytes and both floors. The page judges (it knows the fps being recorded). Latched (`TRANSIENT_LOCAL`, the `activation_agent` QoS) AND re-published at 1 Hz, always (idle, recording, collision), on its own `MutuallyExclusiveCallbackGroup` so a blocking default-group callback cannot stall it; it publishes nothing while `communicator is None`. The arrival counters live in the Communicator (`source_counters()`, bumped first in each camera/follower/leader callback, which run on the sensor node's thread since round 5) and survive `clear_latest_data`. Round 5 added ONE trailing key, `"ingest": {"alive", "age_s"}` (`signal_status.ingest_status`: the sensor thread's loop ran within `INGEST_ALIVE_S`, 1 s): `v` stays 1 and every key before it keeps its order; the page ignores it (a dead thread already shows every source as „steht"). `SOURCE_STOPPED_S` (2 s, the recorder's source-stop rule) equals the page's `utils/signalStatus.js::STALLED_AFTER_S` (`robotis_ai_setup/tests/test_record_r5_lockstep.py`). An older image has no topic, and the page then shows no badge, no banner and no block.
- **D14: a Start never resumes a stale local copy over a newer hub** (Daten 2.0). Before anything else `DataManager._check_dataset_exists` consults the dataset's Daten lease (`_dataset_lease`, injected by the node only when Daten runs; a missing lease or any exception from it is „no lease"): an UPLOAD of this dataset is waited for (`START_UPLOAD_POLL_S`, no timer of its own; FINISH/STOP abandons the Start without an error, `_start_abandoned`), an edit, delete or download refuses with `DATASET_BUSY_START_DE`. With upload on and a COMPLETE local copy, ONE decision against the hub follows (`_resume_against_hub` → `_hub_decision`: every network step in one thread joined after `HUB_CHECK_TIMEOUT_S`, then `dataset_sync.decide`): `current`, `changed`, `local` and `unknown` resume; `newer` (the hub moved, the local copy did not) runs the sync download through the download worker (`_run_sync_download`, waited for the same way, FINISH/STOP cancels it) and then resumes; `conflict` refuses with `SYNC_CONFLICT_DE` (a sync record exists) or `SYNC_UNKNOWN_DE` (no record: which side is newer cannot be told), both sending the student to Daten. The head the decision saw is `_sync_base` and rides the end-of-session upload as `expected_hub_sha` (no key when the Start could not ask), so a hub that moved during the session refuses that upload (`HUB_CHANGED_SINCE_CHECK_DE`, owner decision N5) instead of overwriting it.
- **The sync model is ONE decision** (`data_processing/dataset_sync.py`, stdlib only) for the library badge, the Start and the upload: `decide(root, record, hub) → (state, reason, repair)`. The sync point is main's head commit; „online changed" means the head differs from the recorded one AND the tree ids of `data/`, `meta/`, `videos/` differ (a README-only commit is no change). The record is the SIBLING file `<name>.sync.json` (never inside the dataset, never uploaded): the head, the three tree ids and `files` (the sha256 of every file under `data/`, `meta/episodes/`, `videos/`). A dataset without a record is decided by content: `data/` and `meta/` exactly by the hub's own fields (an LFS file by `lfs.sha256`, since its `blob_id` is the POINTER's id), videos by size; the upload then checks exactly every file that decision assumed equal (`assumed_equal`). The decision itself writes nothing; one that finds such a dataset `current` hands it to the sidecar's remember step (the sidecar bullet), which writes its record on proof, so a later local edit reads `changed` (one upload) instead of a base-less conflict. Our commits carry `[edubotics:<meta digest[:16]>]` FIRST in their title, which is how a lost record write is recognised and repaired. The other siblings are `.<name>.sync.next.json` (a swap's next record), `<name>.session.json` (the crash marker), `.<name>.lock` (the per-stage lock) and `.<name>.journal.json` (a split's journal); `dataset_sync.listed_names` keeps them and every transaction directory (`RESERVED_SUFFIXES`) out of every dataset list, the Training list included.
- **The terminating tick.** The READY tick that ends a recording carries `[WARNUNG]` iff the upload was blocked (`_upload_blocked_reason_de`: finalize failure, namespace refusal, enqueue failure); every other warning rides the SAVING tick before it. The page's finish card reads that tick as „Hochladen fehlgeschlagen" at once.
- **Every dataset upload is ONE guarded commit** (`data_processing/hub_sync.py::upload`, run in the HF worker by `DataManager.upload_huggingface_repo` → `_upload_dataset_guarded`; recording, Daten and the old page alike, so no caller can skip a gate). The old page's `/huggingface/control` upload goes through `DatenService.send_control_upload` → `_enqueue_upload`, the same transient `upload` lease and busy check as a Daten upload, so it never starts on a dataset an edit, delete, download or recording holds (a recording: `BUSY_RECORD_UPLOAD_DE`). A Daten upload asks the same way: `node_service._upload` checks a live `record` lease of that dataset BEFORE the dataset-state checks (a live session has a crash marker), so an upload during its recording answers `busy_record` with `BUSY_RECORD_UPLOAD_DE`, never the crash-marker sentence (R2-4). In order: the cheap local gate before any network call (a crash marker → `in_session`, `UPLOAD_IN_SESSION_DE`); the namespace = the token's ACCOUNT; for a repo that does not exist yet (`repo_exists`) the FULL local gate (`local_gate`, `UPLOAD_BROKEN_DE`) BEFORE `create_repo`, so a broken copy never creates an empty repo (V1-6); `create_repo(exist_ok=True)`; the hub view at main's head and the repo's REAL visibility (the card is written with it); the head check against `expected` (the head the caller's decision saw; `None` = the hub must hold no dataset; absent = decide now and refuse anything but `current`/`changed`/`local` as `hub_differs`, `UPLOAD_HUB_DIFFERS_DE`); the full local gate on the hashes `CommitOperationAdd` computed (every file the record vouches for unchanged, `v3_surgery.integrity` unless it already ran before `create_repo`: `local_broken`, `UPLOAD_BROKEN_DE`; neither refusal sentence assumes an online version, T1-3); the exact check of every file a record-less decision assumed equal; `preupload_lfs_files` per file; then ONE `create_commit(parent_commit=head)` whose operations include the orphan deletes (v3.0 loads parquet by glob). **The returned commit is never trusted**: huggingface_hub's no-op path returns main's head with no parent check, so the hub is read back (`READBACK_TRIES`) and only `new == head` or our marker counts; a commit error is classified by RE-READING the hub (it landed → success; main moved → `hub_changed`), never by a status code; a landed commit whose read-back fails is `UPLOAD_UNCONFIRMED_DE`, never „nothing uploaded". `v3.0` (`move_tag`; LeRobot 0.5.1 trains at `CODEBASE_VERSION='v3.0'`) is moved only to main's CURRENT head while main holds our data; a tag failure after a landed commit is `HUB_TAG_FAILED_DE` and the card reads `changed` until the next upload. `upload_large_folder`/`upload_folder` are never used for a dataset (the model path keeps `upload_large_folder`); the unguarded `push_to_hub` fallback and `_sync_dataset_repo_after_upload` are gone, and without a wired HF worker nothing is uploaded (`UPLOAD_NOT_STARTED_DE`). The status extras (`repo_type`, `info_json`, a success sentence) reach the parent on the progress queue as one `upload_extras` item (`UPLOAD_EXTRAS_WAIT_S` 1 s) and ride `HFOperationStatus.repo_type`/`info_json` to the page; the auto-upload's own failures are the four `AUTO_UPLOAD_*_DE` sentences.
- **Dataset edits are ONE lossless engine** (`data_processing/v3_surgery.py::assemble`, driven by `data_editor_v3`): delete, split, merge and „Beide behalten" build an output of `(source, episode)` parts by STREAM COPY at episode boundaries — data rows verbatim with the three index columns renumbered, video packets cut in DECODE order by the one cutter (`cut_episode`) and re-muxed without a re-encode. A dataset is opened only when its `info.json` carries LeRobot's DEFAULT `data_path`/`video_path` and every feature key matches `FEATURE_KEY_RE`; videos not cut at episode boundaries are refused `unaligned`, and every output is written with the default templates. A merge first runs the seven compatibility checks (`MERGE_CHECKS`: version, robot, fps, cameras, joints, video, stats). Every output is built in `<dataset>.tmp_edit`, verified (`v3_surgery.verify`: lengths, spans, packets, the physical row order, every data column, the per-episode statistics, the first and last frame of every episode decoded at the train-time tolerance), swapped in together with its record (`hub_sync.swap_in`) and re-checked; a split promotes its two outputs under a journal (`recover_split`), all or nothing across a crash. „Beide behalten" is a THREE-WAY plan of the local and the hub copy against the last synced base (`dataset_sync.plan_keep_both` over `v3_surgery.episode_identity`, which is data rows AND video packets); a record-less copy has no base and falls back to the union (an episode deleted on either side comes back; the page's tip says so, `cardModel::keepBothTip`). A „Beide behalten" whose dataset's own sync record already names the hub head it expects (`hub_sha == expected_hub_sha`: nothing changed online, a plain upload is right, and the union would bring this side's deletions back) is refused by `node_service._keep_both` with the command code `stale`, and `_keep_both_job` re-checks it after the download (the base IS the head: the job fails `stale` and the union never runs; R2-2). The page offers „Beide behalten" only on a `conflict` and shows the refusal verbatim. Routing still keys on positive v2.x detection (`data_editor_v3.is_v21_dataset`: `codebase_version.startswith('v2')`), never `not is_v3`. Edits run in `edit_worker.py` at `nice -n 19` from the Daten job runner (one edit-class job at a time) and from the old `/dataset/edit` (through `DatenService.run_edit_blocking` while Daten runs, else the old single-flight `_edit_lock` path, with `EDUBOTICS_DATASET_EDIT_SUBPROCESS=0` its in-process rollback). An edit-class action is refused while its dataset records, uploads, downloads or is edited (the lease), and a dataset in the state `in_session`, `old_format`, `unsupported` or `incomplete` is refused in German.
- **Inference**: `load_policy()` is GPU-gated; SmolVLA on aarch64 is refused at `validate_policy()`/START only (the policy list is unfiltered; the React dropdown filters by `REACT_APP_ALLOWED_POLICIES`). START_INFERENCE publishes `INFERENCE_LOADING(10)` immediately and eager-loads on a daemon thread. Preflight in the node refuses camera-contract mismatch, a language-conditioned policy with empty Aufgabenanweisung, fps ≤ 0, a tick rate differing from the model's training fps (`edubotics_model_meta.json`) and `record_inference_mode`, all in German before any timer starts; `predict()` re-checks the camera contract every tick. `clear_policy()` does `empty_cache()` + `gc.collect()`.
- **HF uploads run in a `multiprocessing` 'spawn' child** (`hf_api_worker.py`) so the child never inherits the rclpy node; a dead-worker check emits one synthetic `Failed` HFOperationStatus. The React client toasts an HFOperationStatus `message` verbatim, so `check_task_status` hands it the worker's own German sentence and every sentence the worker process queues is German; the English wrapper goes to the log only (`tests/test_hf_worker_status_message.py`). **Failures name their cause** (round 5, R5-4b): `data_processing/hf_errors.py::classify_hf_error` walks the exception and its `__cause__`/`__context__` chain (≤ 8) — `LocalTokenNotFoundError` or a 401/403 status → auth (the „Ersetze dein Token auf der Startseite" sentence, `HF_AUTH_ERROR_DE`, byte-equal in `DataManager` and `record_texts_de`), 429 → busy, ≥ 500 → server, an httpx transport error / requests `ConnectionError`/`Timeout` / `ConnectionError` / `TimeoutError` / `socket.gaierror` / the hub's offline mode → network, then the old text markers — recognising library classes by module and name, never importing them; the sentences are `record_texts_de.HF_ERROR_SENTENCES_DE`, and an unclassified failure keeps the caller's generic sentence. **The dataset README is LeRobot's own card** (owner decisions O5/D6, `data_processing/dataset_card.py::build_dataset_card` → `create_lerobot_dataset_card`), rebuilt on EVERY upload so a resumed dataset's README matches its `info.json` (since Daten 2.0 with the repo's REAL visibility, read from the hub before the commit); it names the repo_id, and a PUBLIC dataset's card carries an explicit `license: apache-2.0` (a private one none); our forked template is deleted. Its YAML front matter is load-bearing: `huggingface_hub` validates it (`POST /api/validate-yaml`) before the commit, and an invalid README made `upload_large_folder` retry its commit forever. **An upload that stops moving is ended** (owner decision F7): `upload_large_folder` retries a failing LFS pre-upload or commit forever and its report line does not move during one large file. The child forwards every ERROR record of `huggingface_hub._upload_large_folder` („Failed to preupload LFS / to commit / to get upload mode / to compute sha256") as `{'type': 'upload_error'}` (`progress_tracker.install_upload_error_forwarder`); the parent drains EVERY progress item and `hf_api_worker.UploadStallWatch` calls an upload stalled after `UPLOAD_STALL_S` (120 s) without progress while it logs errors, or `UPLOAD_HARD_STALL_S` (30 min) without progress at all; the child is then killed, ONE `Failed` carries `UPLOAD_STALL_DE` and the node starts a fresh worker on the next request (a DATASET upload, the guarded commit, reports progress after every pre-uploaded file, which feeds the same watch; the error forwarder concerns the model path's `upload_large_folder`); the local dataset is untouched (the worker only reads it). **Every other mode is bounded too** (2026-10-04): `is_busy()` gates every change of the robot's token, so a download with no progress for `DOWNLOAD_STALL_S` (= the upload's hard cap; `snapshot_download` reports per file) and a list fetch or delete running longer than `HUB_QUERY_TIMEOUT_S` (120 s) end the same way (`_fail_stalled_task`, `DOWNLOAD_STALL_DE` / `HUB_QUERY_STALL_DE`). Byte counters cannot replace this: `/proc/<pid>/io` does not count `send()` (4 MB sent, 96 bytes counted), the NIC counters carry video and DDS, and `hf_xet` uploads outside httpx. `Communicator.publish_status()` auto-stamps `robot_type` + `robot_profile`/`capabilities_json` into any empty-typed TaskStatus; the collision monitor's own publisher stamps all three itself (`_stamp_identity`). `rosbag_recorder`'s services are probed at `Communicator` construction (3 s) and retried by a background timer.
- Pre-v0.5.1 checkpoints (no `policy_preprocessor.json`) cannot be loaded; v2.1 datasets fail Modal preflight.

## React manager app (physical_ai_manager)

- **Stack**: React 19 + Redux Toolkit + Vite 8 (`@vitejs/plugin-react-swc`) + vitest + Tailwind. `npm install --legacy-peer-deps`. The app's `package.json` version (0.9.0) is decoupled from the product VERSION.
- **Build invariants**: `build.outDir='build'` + CRA's `static/js|css/[name]-[hash]` layout (all three Dockerfiles `COPY /app/build`, all three nginx configs depend on it). `REACT_APP_*` are inlined as string literals via the `define` shim (the app reads `process.env.*`); all three Dockerfiles hard-fail on empty Supabase/API args; CI greps the bundle for the placeholders at 5 sites (3 in `ci.yml`, 1 in `railway-deploy-teacher-web.yml`, 1 in `docker-publish.yml::smoke-test`). The `.js`→jsx mapping is safe only because there are 0 TypeScript files. Sourcemaps off. `test.include` is pinned to `src/**/*.{test,spec}.{js,jsx}`.
- **No `<React.StrictMode>` in `index.js`** (the dev double-mount races the topic-subscription lifecycle and kills `/task/status`, incl. the collision modal).
- **rosbridge is a module-level singleton** (`utils/rosConnectionManager.js`); an empty URL throws. The local URL is same-origin `ws://<host>/rosbridge` on both platforms through nginx (a blocking test asserts no `9090`). Jetson mode re-points to `:9091` with a JWT auth first-frame.
- **Liveness/recovery**: `useHeartbeatWatchdog` runs app-globally. Robot identity is server-pushed (no client selection); the idle identity tick re-delivers `robot_type`/`robot_profile`/`capabilities_json` ≤2-3 s after any (re)connect. `setTaskStatus` never adopts an empty identity from a bare tick; the `/task/status` handler gates `setTaskInfo` on `isRunning || msg.task_info.task_name`; editability is `topicReceived && phase === READY && !running`. Jetson capabilities are cleared on release (`clearCapabilities`). Roboter Studio activity lives in `state.workshop`, not `tasks.taskStatus`.
- **Enum contracts**: `constants/taskCommand.js` (NONE 0 … FORCE_RESUME_TELEOP 10; the `.srv` names 0 `IDLE`, covered by `NAME_ALIASES` in `enum_parity.py`) and `taskPhases.js` (READY 0 … INFERENCE_LOADING 10) mirror the ROS wire ints; edit only alongside the `.srv`/`.msg` (CI `enum-parity`).
- **The Startseite carries exactly two controls, the activation button and the Hugging-Face-Token card.** The activation button (`Home/ActivationCard.jsx` + `hooks/useRobotActivation.js`) subscribes to `/edubotics/activation_state` and calls `/edubotics/activate`. „Not during a recording/inference" (`taskStatus.running`) and „not while a program runs" (`selectWorkflowRunning`) are decisions this client owns. `status === null` is unknown and still offers the button (an older image has no agent). It renders `null` under a claimed Jetson. `HomePage.test.js` pins it on all four capability fixtures, and keeps the fence that the page carries no „Aufnahme starten". The token card (`Home/HfTokenCard.jsx`, see the account-token bullet) is hidden in cloud-only mode, and an UNKNOWN token state never blocks the start of a recording (owner decision: no `unverified` block); only a positive answer does (`phaseModel.deriveStartBlock` kind `hftoken`: no token stored, unusable, still being transferred, the robot `busy` (recording or talking to Hugging Face, its own sentence, no Startseite link, since 2026-10-04), transfer failed, taken over by another account). An account state that answers nothing usable (`error`, `unavailable`, `unsupported`) on a robot that takes a personal token, and the offline login escape, are a NON-blocking `warn` instead (`hfTokenSelectors::selectHfRecordHint` → `problems.js::hfTokenHintProblem`, row 5c, linking to the Startseite), naming why the Benutzer-ID list is empty, and only while it IS empty (a list loaded earlier stays on screen and usable, so the selector answers null while `state.ui.hfUserList` is non-empty). „taken over" is never derived from a state older than the student's own push: `syncPushed` sets `lastOwnFp` at once (the multi-client simulation diverges otherwise) plus `awaitingOwnFp`/`awaitingUntil`, `decideSync` ignores a foreign slot while that push waits to be SEEN, the reconcile holds still meanwhile, and `useHfTokenSync`'s effect 3b dispatches `syncSettleElapsed` after `WRITE_SETTLE_MS`. The card's input is a masked TEXT field (`-webkit-text-security`, `autoComplete="off"`, the password-manager ignore attributes), falling back to `type="password"` where a text field cannot be masked, so a shared browser profile is not offered to save the token; under the offline escape the card says why the state never loads (`card.offline`).
- **The Startseite (`HomePage.js`) is a report, not a launcher**: the hero (`Home/RobotHero.jsx`), the Health-Check (`Home/HealthCard.jsx`) and „Deine Arbeit" (`Home/WorkCard.jsx`). It dispatches no navigation and carries no CTA (the removed „Aufnahme starten" was disabled on three of the four profiles). **Unknown is not zero anywhere on this page**: `utils/homeHealth.js::deriveHealth` returns UNKNOWN → „—" for anything that has not answered; `hooks/useStudentWork.js` returns `null` counts, never `0`. Data gotchas: `datasets.episode_count` is nullable; `GET /workflows` returns the caller's rows plus the classroom's templates (filter on `owner_user_id` and `!is_template`); `/trainings/list` returns a workgroup's shared rows and `TrainingJob` carries no owner id, so trainings/credits are labelled as the group's. `hooks/useJointLiveness.js` is a separate 1 Hz `/joint_states` subscription, three-state, not folded into `UrdfTwin` (which is unmounted whenever the hero shows the camera). `UrdfTwin` stays `React.lazy` and mounts only while connected (a plain import puts three.js in the entry bundle the white-screen greps watch); the camera view never auto-starts. The view choice persists in `edubotics_home_view` (student-scoped). `utils/productVersion.js` reads the product version from `?_v=<IMAGE_TAG>` and returns null for `latest`/absent.
- **The Aufnahme page (Aufnahme 2.0) is a composition over one logic hook.** A light page in the Startseite language around a dark stage (`StudentApp.js::isDarkPage` is Inferenz only now). `pages/RecordPage.js` only composes; `components/Record/useRecordController.js` is the page's one logic hook (views, buttons, keys, commands, problems, the Benutzer-ID auto-reload/-select ported from the deleted `InfoPanel`; the reload is gated by the `hfToken` state at its one choke point `hooks/useHfUserList.js::reload`, so a student never loads the previous student's list from a leftover robot token; since Daten 2.0 the list holds the token's own ACCOUNT only, and a stored Benutzer-ID it does not contain — an organisation chosen before 2026-10-05 — is replaced by the account, R-10); the decisions are pure models under `components/Record/model/` (`phaseModel`, `recordCommands`, `recordKeys`, `problems`, `finishModel`), every German string is `model/recordCopy.js`, and the fifteen `components/Record/*.jsx` are presentational (props only; `CameraTiles` gets its service call injected). Rules that hold it together:
  - **Timed phases only**, drawn from the wire: warm-up/reset overlays with a countdown ring (inline SVG, `PhaseOverlay.jsx` is in `INLINE_SVG_ALLOWED`), „Los!" only on a RECORDING entry seen while mounted, the red recording frame, the Speichern chips (the saving chip ≥ 400 ms, the saved chip 1200 ms per new saved row; no progress bar, `encoding_progress` is always 0), the Phasenleiste and the Episodenleiste. Frame-rate movement goes through `hooks/useSmoothPhaseClock`'s `subscribe` into refs, never React state. **The page does not re-render per /task/status tick**: `RecordPage` is `React.memo`, `StudentApp` reads only the task fields it uses (`running`, `topicReceived`, `taskType`, and the stable `robotType`/`capabilities`; never the whole `taskStatus`/`taskInfo`, which are replaced with every ~30 Hz tick), the tasks reducer keeps `taskInfo`'s reference when the adopt path re-sends the same values (arrays compared by content), and `features/tasks/recordSelectors.js::selectRecordStatus`/`selectRecordForm` return the same object while nothing in them changed (`receivedAt` is left out). The page renders only when a value it shows changes: a few times a second (measured in a browser with the status feeder: 4–6/s while recording, ~2.6/s idle; the round-4 K1 end-to-end run on the real stack 7.3/s warm-up, 6.1/s recording, 7.35/s reset, 3.35/s idle, i.e. 6–9/s in the timed phases and ~3/s idle; the first version of this page measured ~60/s); identical ticks render nothing (`components/Record/__tests__/renderCount.test.jsx`: 0 renders over 30 ticks, 30 without the memo).
  - **Commands** (`recordCommands::runRecordAction`) re-check their precondition on the LATEST status and send nothing when it no longer holds; STOP and SKIP_TASK are never sent; there are no success toasts, and a refused „Jetzt starten/Jetzt weiter/Jetzt speichern" is silent (F4, the phase display is the truth). Every RECORD-page command sends `utils/recordTaskInfo.js::forceRecordTaskInfo` (streaming save, no rosbag, one instruction; H7). Validation runs on the click. Start stays disabled from the click to the first record tick or error tick, with no give-up (F2; after 8 s „Dauert länger als gewohnt …"). „Beenden" during a running episode asks („Behalten und beenden" → FINISH, „Verwerfen und beenden" → RERECORD then FINISH, „Weiter aufnehmen"/Esc); Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+X opens that question and never ends an episode on its own. Key labels are words („Leertaste", „Strg+Umschalt+X"), never ⇧/⌘. „Beenden" is disabled during a collision (F6c); the CollisionModal is unchanged and the stage only shows what happened behind it. **The robot's busy refusal is never silent** (round 5, O6): a command answered with `BUSY_DE` (`recordCommands::isBusyAnswer`, the constant imported from `features/tasks/recordSession.js`) shows that sentence, F4's silence covers only real refusals; and in „Verwerfen und beenden" a busy RERECORD aborts the sequence (no FINISH, the intent cleared), because FINISH after a RERECORD that discarded nothing would KEEP the take the student threw away. A FINISH the robot queued (round 6, answered success with `FINISH_QUEUED_DE`) is plain success: no banner, the end intent stays. **Known, accepted (owner decision, round 7):** „Verwerfen und beenden" within ~1 s after „Wiederholen" meets a RERECORD that answers busy (the first discard is still running), so the page sends no FINISH and the retake is saved; workaround: wait a moment or press „Beenden" again (`docs/KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). Round 7 on the page: behind the CollisionModal the stage card agrees with the row — a save-window collision's „Gespeichert" row (`inCollision`) makes it „Episode N wurde vor der Kollision gespeichert …" (`useRecordController`'s `copy.card.collisionBody`), and when that row settles counted on the LAST episode the session ends normally, so `collisionOpen` clears and no „nach der Kollision beendet" note is added; a failed or never-begun upload skips step 3; the upload-off reason is the finish card's note (said once — the banner leaves it out) and the upload step only says it was off; with „Datensatz unvollständig" the saved rows read „Gespeichert, Datensatz unvollständig"; the header pill's late-heartbeat state is „Zeitüberschreitung" (`components/HeartbeatStatus.js`). The local same-origin rosbridge (`utils/rosConnectionManager.js::isLocalRosbridgeUrl`: path `/rosbridge`, not the Jetson proxy's :9091) reconnects after `min(1000·2^n, LOCAL_RECONNECT_MAX_DELAY_MS = 2000)` ms with no attempt cap, so a page that lost its link during a recording sees the session's end (the idle identity tick → `linkLost`) within ~4 s instead of up to ~30 s; the Jetson keeps the 30-attempt exponential backoff.
  - **`[WARNUNG]` handling is record-tick-only** (`task_type === 'record'`): the text becomes a page notice (`tasks.recordNotice`), and `taskStatus.error` stays `''` for every dispatched tick, so the HealthCard, the Inferenz page and the Jetson run closing behave as before (F3).
  - **One banner, only for a real problem** (`model/problems.js`, the first one wins), from `/edubotics/signal_status` via `hooks/useSignalStatus`; a stalled source or the disk floor refuses Start with its reason (Q5: the un-activated leader links to the Startseite, activation stays there), and the tile rate badges exist only while the topic does. Vocabulary „Leader-Arm"/„Follower-Arm" (never Leitarm/Folgearm) and „Greifer-Kamera"/„Szenen-Kamera" (F6a).
  - **Session and finish live in `tasks`** (no new slice): `phaseAnchor` is the rig's and survives sign-out, `recordSession` (rules R1–R17 in `features/tasks/recordSession.js`) and `recordNotice` are the student's and are reset by `session/signedOut`. „Diese Sitzung" survives a tab switch, not a reload. Round-5 row rules (`recordSession.js`): a collision in the save window settles its provisional „Gespeichert" on the first record tick that COUNTED it (any phase, SAVING included — the last episode is counted while the robot is still saving) or is not SAVING, before R3, so the increment is consumed once; a SAVING tick with the old count leaves it provisional (a pre-trip tick delivered late across the two `/task/status` publishers). „Behalten und beenden" measures the run to the PRESS (the server judges it at press + RTT, so a run the client measures ≥ 1 s is always kept). Two outcomes beyond saved/redo/collision/drop/ended: `'source'` („Abgebrochen, verworfen": the session ended with an unresolved run, no page intent, and a warning within `END_NOTE_WINDOW_MS` starting `SOURCE_STOP_PREFIX_DE` „Aufnahme beendet: " — a stopped source or C7) and `'gap'` („Signalaussetzer, wiederholt": SAVING → RESETTING, or the H15 SAVING → RECORDING, without a count after a warning starting `SOURCE_GAP_PREFIX_DE`); round 6 adds `'gap_end'` („Signalaussetzer, verworfen": a gap in the take „Beenden" ended — discarded, not re-recorded). The Q8 reason survives a fast end in either arrival order (`note.nothingSavedAfterRedo`). Round 6 finish rules: the error-stop card reads what the robot said about the dataset (`model/finishModel.js::errorStopParts`): `ERROR_STOP_SAVED_DE` → step 1 ✓, the upload skipped with where to do it, „Auf diesem Rechner gespeichert als"; `ERROR_STOP_INCOMPLETE_DE` → „Datensatz unvollständig" with step 1 ✗; neither → nothing claimed (skipped, or unknown when an older robot had saved episodes) — never a red ✗ beside „gesichert" (F3). A record `[WARNUNG]` starting `UPLOAD_OFF_PREFIX_DE` (also one on the very first record tick) marks the session as running without upload (`recordSession.uploadOff`, F4): the save name says „Hochladen ist für diese Aufnahme aus (kein gültiger Hugging-Face-Token) …", the finish is `local_done` with the robot's reason on the upload step, and that notice on the terminating tick is not a blocked upload (a finalize failure still is). A failure sentence that already sends the student to the Daten tab („… im Tab Daten hochladen", „Öffne den Tab Daten …", „Lösche ihn im Tab Daten …") is not followed by a second „später hochladen" (`model/finishModel.js::POINTS_TO_DATEN_TAB`, G-9: after a hub that moved, „lade später hoch" would nudge an overwrite). These shared strings are byte-equal py↔js (`tests/test_record_r5_lockstep.py`). The finish card has three steps (close the dataset, upload, enter it in the list), each done, running, failed, skipped or unknown with its own mark (`features/tasks/recordSession.js` + `model/finishModel.js`): a terminating `[WARNUNG]` that starts „Datensatz konnte nicht abgeschlossen werden" (or any, with upload off) is `finalize_failed` — step 1 failed, nothing „bereit", only „Neue Aufnahme" — and a failed or skipped registration is never a green check. After a session the form stays locked with its own reason (`lockedKind: 'finished'`, „Die Aufnahme ist beendet …") until „Neue Aufnahme", which dismisses the card while upload tracking continues; the HF toast is suppressed only while that card is visible.
  - **STARTING names an upload of the same dataset, and only then listens** (Daten 2.0, R-8): `features/editDataset/hooks/useDatenStartWait` subscribes to `/edubotics/daten_state` ONLY while the view is STARTING and returns one boolean that changes only when the answer does (30 daten_state messages in STARTING → 0 renders, `renderCount.test.jsx`). While the robot uploads the dataset the Start is for (`datasetRepoId` of `pendingStart.snapshot`; the session's own snapshot does not exist yet in STARTING), the pill says „Wartet, bis das Hochladen fertig ist …" (`recordCopy.pill.waitUpload`), after 8 s the existing „Dauert länger als gewohnt …". Words only: the robot waits by itself (D14). No topic (an older image) → nothing changes. **The page never refuses that Start** (owner decision V2-3): `model/phaseModel.js::deriveStartBlock` knows only the disk, a stalled source and the student's token, so „Neue Aufnahme" → Start right after a session reaches this wait from the same tab (a „same dataset still uploads" block once made it unreachable), and the running upload's finish stays tracked across the new Start (`recordSession`'s carry). `useDatenStartWait::isUploading` asks for ANY `upload` entry of the id (`useDatenState::busyKindsById`), because the robot lists a waiting Start as `record` AND `upload` and no reader may depend on which comes last (C-2).
  - **Layout** (`components/Record/record.css`, everything `rec-`): the page is the `rec` size container. The problem banner and the action bar are ONE footer group (`.rec-footer`), a flow sibling after the stage, never absolute or fixed. ≤ 1150 px of page (1093 px at 125 %) the page is one scrolling column and that footer is sticky at the bottom of its column on the page's own background, so the banner can never slide under the bar; a banner takes its room from the stage; entering a session scrolls back to the stage. Stage cards centre their content with auto margins (never plain flex centring), so a card taller than the stage starts at its top and scrolls; on a narrow page the finish card joins the flow, the stage grows with it and the footer is not sticky then (the bar has no command to offer, and it would cover the card's buttons). **The action bar sheds only when its row really does not fit**, measured after layout (`ActionBar.jsx::barFitsOneRow`: no horizontal overflow and one row of its normal height), and only with THREE command buttons (recording / saving): first Beenden's „Strg+Umschalt+X" label, then the other key labels, then the dots' label, then the dots' size. Measured: at 1440 px while recording the key labels go (plus the dots' label from five dots), at 1366 px also the dots' label, at 1093 px only as much as the dot count needs. With fewer buttons nothing is shed and the dots may wrap onto a second line, like the mockup. The shortcuts keep working, stay in `aria-keyshortcuts` and are named in the button title. **A mouse click never leaves the focus on a button** (`ActionBar.jsx::releasePointerFocus`, on every button of the bar, the switches, the steppers, „Erweitert", „Neu laden" and the finish actions, and — through `StudentApp.js::navClick` — on the sidebar and bottom-navigation buttons, so arriving on the page by mouse leaves no nav button focused): Space means „Aufnahme starten", and the browser would otherwise press the last-clicked button again; a keyboard click (`detail === 0`) keeps the focus, so Tab navigation and its focus ring are unchanged. The stage view („Kameras" | „3D" | „Beides") is the MACHINE key `edubotics_record_view`; the lazy `UrdfTwin` mounts only for 3D/Beides while connected, with `showChrome={false}` and the additive `viewPreset` („Perspektive/Vorne/Seite/Oben", not persisted), which moves the camera through the stored `requestRenderRef` (`needsRender` is local to the mount effect) and is re-applied after the framing pass unless the student orbited. Camera tiles use `ImageGridCell`'s `bare` prop (and its optional `onStreamError`); the topic list is re-asked when the link returns and while the last answer failed or lacked a camera.
- **The Daten tab (Daten 2.0) is a composition over a few logic hooks, pure models and one copy module, and the player never re-renders per frame.** `pages/EditDatasetPage.js` is thin (it caps the page's toasts at 3); `components/Daten/DatenPage.jsx` orchestrates the library (`LibraryView`, `DatasetCard`, `MergePanel`), the player (`PlayerView`, `React.memo`, with `Stage`/`Transport`/`Scrubber`/`JointCharts`/`HintsPanel`/`DetailsPanel`/`EpisodeList`) and the dialogs under `components/Daten/dialogs/`; everything under `components/Daten/` is presentational. The logic is `features/editDataset/hooks/`: `useDatenCommand` (the one `/daten/command` caller, normalised to `{ok, code, message, result, oldImage, unreachable}`; a service that „does not exist" is an OLDER IMAGE, which the page names instead of failing), `useDatenSession` (link tokens, the library, re-fetches), `useDatenJobs`, `useDatenState` (ONE ref-counted `/edubotics/daten_state` subscription for the whole app), `useEpisodePlayer`, `useGroupNamespaces` (see the cloud's `GET /me/group-members`) and `useDatasetLocalChanges` (the Training page's warning). The decisions are pure modules in `features/editDataset/model/`, every German string is `datenCopy.js` (templates filled by `model/format.js::fill`), and the wire constants of both sides are `datenContract.js`, a verbatim copy of the spec's shared table (the server holds the Python twin). Media and data come over HTTP from the robot's sidecar (`/daten-api/v1`, `api/datenHttp.js`: a link token per library or dataset, a JSON 503 retried after its `Retry-After` at most `JSON_503_RETRIES` (2) times, thumbnails and clips with their own delays, a refused token re-minted ONCE). Rules that hold it together:
  - **The library merges by proof, never by arrival order**: each dataset id carries its own request sequence (a late reply for an older request never overwrites a newer one), a reply asked without the hub never replaces the hub half already shown, and a hub half stamped with another token's fingerprint (`token_fp`) is dropped (`model/libraryState.js`). A card's kind comes from `model/cardModel.js` alone.
  - **The player's clock is the video**: `useEpisodePlayer::createEpisodeEngine` drives time from `requestVideoFrameCallback` (rAF + a virtual clock where it is missing), seeks to the middle of a frame (`(i + 0.5) / fps`), steps by frame index, re-syncs the other cameras only on a measured drift, and publishes through refs and subscribers — never React state per frame. **Measured, not assumed**: the build key `REACT_APP_DATEN_RENDER_PROBE=1` (in the vite `define` list, off in every shipped build) makes `features/editDataset/renderProbe.js` count renders in `window.__datenRenders`; 100 driver frames render `PlayerView`, `Stage`, `Transport`, `JointCharts` and `EpisodeList` 0 times, and `daten_state` messages do not reach the memoised player either (stable props). Never measure it with React's Profiler.
  - **Marks belong to one version of a dataset**: `editDataset.marks` is keyed by id AND `meta_digest` (`selectMarks(id, digest)`); a dataset that changed underneath drops them. **„Beide behalten" is every conflict's default** (first and focused in `dialogs/ConflictChoices.jsx`). It keeps every NEW episode of both sides; what either side deleted or replaced since the last sync stays gone (G-2), and no sentence may promise more: `datenCopy.keepBoth.tip` carries the core sentence („„Beide behalten“ behält alle neuen Episoden von hier und von Hugging Face. Was seit dem letzten Abgleich auf einer Seite gelöscht oder ersetzt wurde, bleibt weg."), fenced by `model.test.js`. A dataset with no recorded sync (`local.record.hub_sha` absent) has no base and is merged as the union, so its card, banner and both compare dialogs say `keepBoth.tipNoBase` instead (deleted episodes come back; `cardModel::keepBothTip`, the `tip` of `ConflictChoices`, T2-1). **Only that per-dataset tip says what „Beide behalten" keeps**: the robot's `HUB_CHANGED_SINCE_CHECK_DE` (the Daten upload toast and the Aufnahme finish card, for a dataset with OR without a record) promises nothing about it and sends the student to the Daten tab, where the tip stands (R2-1; pinned verbatim by `test_daten_texts_de.py` and `finishModel.test.js`).
  - **Transfers the page started are the page's to announce**: they are remembered in `editDataset.transfers[repoId]`, and `useRosTopicSubscription` does not toast them while the Daten page is open (off the page it toasts the robot's sentence and forgets the transfer). After a successful upload the hook registers a dataset in the cloud registry as before, but never a MODEL upload and never a PRIVATE Daten upload (the platform token cannot read it, so the registration would always fail); the registration-failed toast is skipped while the Aufnahme finish card shows that repo, like the HF toast.
  - **What a card, a banner or a toast says is what the robot last said, never a render-old copy or a guess.** `useDatenSession::getLib()` is the library as of the last applied reply (the reducer run eagerly beside React's), and every job's `onDone` gets whether its re-read landed: the delete toast decides from that re-read (else `libraryState::syncAfterLocalEdit` — an edit turns a synced copy `changed`, a newer one `conflict`) and offers „Jetzt hochladen" only for `changed`. `busyKindsById` keeps every busy kind per id and `libraryState::cardBusyKind` lets the running job beat a waiting `record`; `busyChanges` counts only a lost kind. A re-read of named ids that leaves a „Nur online" card without numbers (the robot reads an online-only `info.json` only for the whole list) reads the whole list at once (`onlineCardsWithoutNumbers`), and the card says „Angaben werden von Hugging Face geladen …" meanwhile; the header names no count before the first reply. A failed online list keeps the robot's `unknown/unreachable` (its tooltip), only foreign or unusable verdicts read „not asked" (`blindSync`). The upload dialog tells `hub: null` + decided `local` (not there yet: the visibility line, `expected_hub_sha: null`) from „could not ask" (no line, no key; `UploadCompareDialog::hubKnowledge`). The newer/changed banners and the newerWarn dialog take the hub copy's count and date from one `hubstate` read per dataset, hub head and local version (the library lists a local dataset's hub entry without numbers); without it the sentences go without numbers, never with „–". A download's bytes are shown in the total's unit with the percentage of the numbers shown (`format.js::fmtBytesProgress`) and never run backwards (`useDatenJobs::withMonotonicBytes`: the robot counts the tmp's bytes, which read 0 for a moment when the finished tmp is swapped in). A crashed or broken card carries no sync badge (it cannot be uploaded), and the PLAYER of a dataset whose card is `live`, `crashed` or `broken` shows that card's sentence and actions instead of the tools, the sync banners, the episodes and the keys, asking for no summary (`PlayerView::PLAYER_BLOCKED_KINDS`); a blocked player opens again only after the robot's re-read (`nextPlayerShown`: the busy change that ends a recording renders once with the reply from before it, T2-2). **Unknown is never 0**: `format.js::knownNumber`/`fmtKnown` turn null, empty and non-numeric values into „–" (`Number(null)` is 0) for the card's stats, the fetch dialog, both compare dialogs, the player's size and the whole-delete toast, and a `hub=0` reply keeps the hub verdict of a dataset that was never local, so an online card the hub could not read just now stays „Online-Stand unbekannt" (T2-5). A copy being fetched shows the numbers the fetch dialog found while it downloads (`DatenPage` keeps them per target, `card.fetchNumbers`, read by `cardModel` after the local and hub numbers; T2-4). A name built on a dataset („… gesamt", „… Teil 2") is its display name or its task part, never the folder name with its robot prefix (`labels.js::taskNameOf`).
  - **`daten.css`'s page reset carries no class weight** (`:where(.dat-page) button, :where(.dat-page) input`): at `.dat-page button` (0,1,1) it beat every `.dat-btn…` rule, leaving dark ink on teal below WCAG AA (`datenCss.test.js` checks the computed cascade). The card's buttons wrap inside `.dat-card-btns`; the ⋮ menu never shrinks and never lands on a row of its own.
  - The slice state (marks, transfers, view, filter) is the student's: `editDataset` answers `signedOut` with its `initialState`; the page writes no browser storage.
- **Modes/tab gating** (four chained filters): `?cloud=1` disables rosbridge/heartbeat and hides `hardwareOnly` tabs; a connected Jetson hides `jetsonIncompatible` tabs; `utils/navGating.js::isCapabilityVisible` hides a `capabilityKey`-tagged tab only on an explicit `false` in `taskStatus.capabilities` (RECORD→recordable, TRAINING→trainable, EDIT_DATASET→editable, WORKSHOP→roboter_studio), skipped when `jetsonConnected`; `piOnly` reveals the System tab only in Orange-Pi mode. Inferenz carries no `capabilityKey`. The Daten tab (EDIT_DATASET, icon `database`) is `hardwareOnly` + `jetsonIncompatible` + `editable` (Daten 2.0: everything it shows comes from the robot's sidecar, so there is nothing to show in `?cloud=1`). `requireRobotOrRedirect` (→ `robotGateDecision`) passes debug and cloud mode through; on hardware it holds until a non-empty `robotType` arrives. The Benutzer-ID list is `state.ui.hfUserList`, selection persisted in `localStorage edubotics_userId`.
- **The student login gate: `StudentApp`'s `<main>` renders `LoginForm` unless `state.auth.isAuthenticated`, with exactly two carve-outs.** The decision is the pure `utils/authGate.js::studentAuthGateDecision({isAuthLoading, isAuthenticated, offlineOverride, piMode, page}) → 'app'|'loading'|'login'`, with a source-text fence in `__tests__/authGate.test.js`. Carve-out 1, non-negotiable: `piMode && page === PageType.SYSTEM` is never gated, checked first (that tab is the whole setup wizard and the only repair surface; `StartupGate` carves out the same page; `?cloud=1` gets none). Carve-out 2: an attempt-gated offline escape („Ohne Anmeldung fortfahren") only after a login attempt has PROVEN the auth service unreachable (`isCloudUnreachableAuthError`: a 4xx is the service judging this request, so no escape; everything else means "we could not ask"). `TrainingPage`/`InferencePage` keep their own `LoginForm` gates. The gate lives inside `<main>` (`CollisionModal` and `PiUpdateGate` are siblings outside it) and keys on `isAuthenticated` and nothing else. Every ordinary recording auto-registers in the cloud dataset registry (`registerUploadedDataset`); „Arme scannen" costs a re-login; a reload drops `offlineOverride`. `german_detail_lint.py` cannot see JSX, so German here is enforced by review.
- **Pi mode**: detected once at boot from the static `/pi-mode.json` marker baked into the opi image (`utils/piMode.js` `PiModeProvider`; requires a valid JSON parse of `{pi:true}`). Live agent health is a separate `/api/system/status` probe (System window only). **`PiUpdateGate` forces the update on a stale Pi**: app-global, self-gated on `piMode`, probes the read-only `GET /api/system/update/check` once and shows a non-closable German modal whose skip appears only after a failed attempt; `hooks/useAgentUpdate.js` drives both surfaces. `StartupGate` never blocks globally in Pi mode; its timeout screen says „Verbindung zum Roboter blockiert? Netzwerk-Anleitung prüfen" (`PI_PORT_BLOCKED_HINT`). `rsControlBase(piMode)` routes the control bridge: `/api/system` in Pi mode, `http://localhost:8769` on Windows.
- **Port-80 consolidation (Pi mode): every browser↔robot transport is same-origin on :80.** `nginx.opi.conf.template` proxies rosbridge (`location = /rosbridge`) and web_video_server (`location = /video/stream`, an exact match deliberately not a prefix, because `handle_stream_viewer` reflects the raw `topic`); variable `proxy_pass` + `resolver 127.0.0.11` defer resolution to request time (the robot tier is down at manager boot). React derives both URLs through `localRosbridgeUrl` and `videoStreamBase`. `StudentApp`'s `piModeResolved` seed gate is vestigial and kept. The opi host-publishes of `:9090`/`:8080` are debug-only and loopback-pinned (`127.0.0.1:`, deliberately not `${EDUBOTICS_BIND_HOST}`; `check_origin` is literally `return True`); debug goes through `ssh -L`. `EDUBOTICS_BIND_HOST` drives the manager's `:80`; `LAN_OPEN=0` is kiosk mode, not a hardening knob (`tests/test_rosbridge_origin_gate.py::OpiDebugPublishesAreLoopbackPinned`). The Pi's Origin rule is host-relative (`$http_host`), constrained by the `$edubotics_host_is_own` map (localhost, IPv4/bracketed-IPv6 literal, a single-label name, anything under `.local`) answering 421 on `/rosbridge` only. `location /api/system/` must `proxy_hide_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin` (the agent's `_cors` sets `ACAO: *` on every JSON response and GETs are not Origin-gated). **`location /daten-api/`** (Daten 2.0, in BOTH `nginx.conf` and `nginx.opi.conf.template`, never the teacher-web config) proxies the dataset sidecar (`physical_ai_server:8095`, variable `proxy_pass` + the existing `resolver`) GET/HEAD only (`limit_except`), unbuffered, with `proxy_hide_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin`; it is TOKEN-gated by the sidecar (a link token minted over `/daten/command`) and deliberately not Origin-gated, because a `<video>`/`<img>` GET carries no Origin. It answers `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, so a document opened at a token URL (a clip or a JSON answer in a tab of its own) never sends that URL on as a Referer; since that is an `add_header` of its own, nothing inherits from the server level, and the block re-declares the other three security headers (`X-Frame-Options`, `X-Content-Type-Options`, `Permissions-Policy`) unchanged (R2-3; `datenNginx.test.js` compares the block's set with the server's). It writes no log at all: every URL there carries a 30-minute link token in its PATH, so `access_log off;` and `error_log /dev/null;` (nginx appends the request line to every error it logs for a request: a refused method, a too-large body, the sidecar down or unresolvable; a failing sidecar still shows on the page and in the server's own log), and the SERVER logs in its own token-free format (`log_format edubotics_tokenless`, the image's `main` with the maps `$edubotics_log_request`/`$edubotics_log_referer` replacing any request line or Referer that names the Daten API, in any case), because a request nginx answers before it picks a location (a header or URI too large, a bad request line, TRACE) or a `/DATEN-API/` spelling that falls through to the SPA is logged by the server (T1-1, measured on nginx 1.27.5 with a real token over 35 request shapes in fix round 2 and 88 in its verification: none in the access log, and in the error log only the residual that follows). **One residual (R2-3, accepted):** nginx's `error_log` format is fixed and ends in `referrer: "…"`, so a request that carries a Daten URL as its Referer BY HAND (its sender already holds the token) and fails in ANOTHER location (measured: `/video/stream` with web_video_server down) writes that token into the error log; the access log redacts it, and the page itself never sends such a Referer. A refused method answers 403 from `limit_except`; a location `return 405` cannot replace it, because `limit_except` swaps the location config before the rewrite phase (measured). `datenNginx.test.js` reads both configs.
- **Browser storage is partitioned STUDENT vs MACHINE, and `utils/sessionScope.js` is the registry.** STUDENT (10): `edubotics_userId`, `edubotics_trainingInfo`, `edubotics_workshop_tempo`, `edubotics_workshop_code_open`, `edubotics_code_last_file` (the file last open in a code program; deliberately not `edubotics_workshop_code_open_file`, which would read as a suffix of the unrelated Code-Vorschau toggle), `edubotics:workshop:theme`, `edubotics_home_view`, and the three multiselect clipboard keys `blocklyStashMulti`/`blocklyStashConnection`/`blocklyStashTime` (written by a plugin the editor no longer loads; scrubbed anyway, and `bootScrub.crossTab.test.js` is the one test that reads `node_modules`). MACHINE (7): `edubotics_robotType`, `edubotics_audio_muted`, the four `edubotics_workshop_dock_*` keys, `edubotics_record_view` (the Aufnahme stage's „Kameras | 3D | Beides"; it replaced `edubotics_urdf_open`). IGNORED (5, each with a written reason in the frozen `IGNORED_STORAGE_KEYS` map): `__edubotics_version_reload_at`, `edubotics_boot_scrub`, `edubotics.jetson.reconnect`, the IndexedDB `edubotics:workshop:autosave`, `edubotics_workshop_autosave_session`. `useAutosave` namespaces every bucket (`:<supabase user id>` signed in, `:<browser-session id>` not; `autosaveSessionScope()` + `SESSION_SCOPE_KEY` stay). An allowlist of literal names, never a prefix sweep (`sessionScope.test.js` fails on any `edubotics*` key in none of the three); the Supabase session key is the one thing matched by pattern (`clearSupabaseSessionKeys`), with two callers: the sign-out path (when a revoke reported failure) and `bootScrub`. When adding a Blockly/editor plugin, check what it persists.
- **Handover between students is a spawn-time scrub in the SPA.** The GUI stamps `?fresh=<nonce>`; `utils/bootScrub.js` answers with `clearStudentScopedStorage()` + `clearSupabaseSessionKeys()`. Four load-bearing properties: a side-effect import placed first in `index.js` (`bootScrubOnLoad`; `store/store.js` and `lib/supabaseClient.js` read localStorage at module evaluation); the latch stores the nonce, not a boolean; a scrub that throws does not latch; it must not call `supabase.auth.signOut()`. Every GUI path that ends a session closes the child first (`_on_close`, `_stop_environment`, `_run_prerequisite_checks_body`, `_scan_arms._do_scan`, enumerated by `test_shutdown_teardown.py`). Those closes are the DELIBERATE ones: `destroy_all` marks its Popen and each is followed by a container stop or an exit, so only a window that ends on its own triggers the robot-token clear (a new closer that stops nothing must clear explicitly).
- **Sign-out is one primitive, the `utils/signOut::signOutStudent` thunk; everything local completes before the first remote await.** Order: `features/jetson/sessionReset::resetJetsonOnLogout` (needs the still-valid JWT) → `features/hfToken/robotChannel::clearRobotHfToken()` STARTED (not awaited: the robot forgets this student's token; skipped on a claimed Jetson; a clear the robot refuses because it is recording or uploading is remembered by the node and applied once it is idle, since 2026-10-04) → `dispatch(signedOut())` → scrub → awaited `supabase.auth.signOut()` → the clear awaited for at most `CLEAR_BOUND_MS` (2 s, errors swallowed) → scrub again → reload. There are two awaits (the revoke, then the bounded robot clear started before it); each is a window (one `/task/status` tick re-adopted the previous id), hence broadcast-first and the second scrub after both. The revoke's return value is read: `@supabase/auth-js::_signOut` skips `_removeSession()` for most admin-signOut failures with nothing thrown, so a failure falls back to `clearSupabaseSessionKeys`. The in-memory half is one broadcast, `session/signedOut` (`features/session/sessionActions`), answered by exactly ten slices in their own `extraReducers`: `auth`, `tasks`, `training`, `editDataset`, `workshop`, `studioAssets`, `teacher`, `admin`, `ui` (it clears `hfUserList`: the Benutzer-ID list describes the signed-in person's token, not the machine) and `hfToken`. The two hydrating slices (`taskSlice`, `trainingSlice`) must reset to snapshot-free defaults (`defaultTaskInfo`, `defaultTrainingState`+`defaultTrainingInfo`), never their `initialState` (module-load localStorage snapshots); `admin`/`editDataset`/`teacher` correctly use `initialState`; `workshop` is selective (calibration is the rig's and stays). `sessionScope.test.js`'s exemption list has exactly two entries (`ros`, `jetson`). `reload: false` is passed by the three paths showing a German toast (`StudentApp`'s and `WebApp`'s wrong-role bounces + `useMeProfile`'s 401); `signOut.test.js` proves the property against the real root reducer. Closing the window without „Abmelden" never runs this thunk; the Windows GUI then asks the node to clear the same slot (see the Windows GUI section).
- **„Abmelden" is refused while an activity is in flight, and the gate needs a live rosbridge for every branch.** `utils/signOut::logoutBlockReason` (`'task' | 'workflow' | 'calibration' | null`) + `LOGOUT_BLOCK_TITLES_DE`; `StudentApp` wires `taskStatus.running`, `selectWorkflowRunning` and `selectCalibrationHasUnsolvedCaptures` (ANDed with `!calibrated` and being on the Roboter-Studio page), all behind `heartbeatStatus === 'connected'`.
- **The robot must not name a student who is not signed in.** `useRosTopicSubscription` gates the adopt of `task_info.user_id` on an identity comparison: `robotNamesMe` = a non-empty `auth.hfUsername` that equals `msg.task_info.user_id`; "is anybody signed in?" is no answer. „Abmelden" is disabled while `taskStatus.running` and, once `handoverStarted`, stays on screen.
- **Lazy chunks keep heavy deps out of the entry bundle**: `UrdfTwin` (three 0.184 + urdf-loader 0.12.7, exact pins, enforced by an eslint `no-restricted-imports` rule), `CodeView`/`pythonCodeGen` (`import('blockly/python')`), all `@blockly/*` plugins (evaluated before the first `Blockly.inject`, see the Blockly-canvas bullet), CodeMirror, and the code insertion (`code/codeInsert.js`, its structure analysis and Java statement parser: statically imported only by the lazy `CodeEditor.jsx`, loaded on demand by `codeAssetDocument.loadCodeInsert`, whose `insertSnippet`/`insertProgram` therefore return a promise the drawer and Vormachen await; a failed load is not cached and says so in German; fenced by `code/__tests__/codeInsert.lazy.test.js`). What the coding suite still keeps in the entry (`codeAssetUsage`, `codeProject`, `codeMessagesDe`, `codeAssetDocument`, `sammlung/assetDocument` + `newActions`) is read synchronously by entry UI: the drawer's counts and usage rows, RunControls' save-first replay scan, the page creating the code document. `components/icons` is in the entry too (the pages are not lazy): named imports from one registry, `react-icons` being `sideEffects: false`, so only the registered icons ship (104 Lucide icons, not the 1541 of the set). Because the lazy `CodeEditor.jsx` imports `icons/svg` as well, rolldown splits React itself into a shared `jsx-runtime-*.js` chunk, which `index.html` modulepreloads.
- **CodeMirror has exactly two importers, and eslint keeps it that way.** `package.json`'s `no-restricted-imports` bans `codemirror`, `@codemirror/*` and `@lezer/*` under `src/` except `components/Workshop/code/CodeEditor.jsx` (student editor) and `components/teacher/CodeViewer.jsx` (teacher's read-only view), both reached through `React.lazy`. Exact pins: `codemirror` 6.0.2, `@codemirror/lang-python` 6.2.1, `@codemirror/lang-java` 6.0.2 (`@codemirror/lint` arrives through `codemirror`). `parseMarkers.js` takes the Lezer `Tree` and CodeMirror `Text` as ARGUMENTS so it stays pure. **A naive `grep -l codemirror build/static/js/*.js` names the entry chunk and always has** (the app bundles `package.json` for the nav version, so its dependency list and these eslint messages are entry-chunk TEXT); measure CodeMirror's runtime tokens (`cm-content` / `cm-scroller` / `cm-editor`), which are zero there.
- **One icon style: Lucide through `components/icons`, never an emoji or symbol glyph in UI text** (owner decision D7). `registry.js` maps a semantic name (what the icon MEANS, never what it looks like: `cancel`, `failed`, `liveRecording`) to a component and is the only importer of `react-icons/lu`; `custom.js` adds four Lucide-style `GenIcon` trees (`robotArm`, `leaderArm` — a closed handle grip where the follower's claw is open —, `python`, and `cloudSynced`, a cloud with a check for the Daten card's „Aktuell" badge: 24×24, no fill, stroke 2, round caps and joins, coordinates in [1, 23], filling the grid like Lucide, bounding box ≥ 16 units, `icons.test.jsx`). **Every registry entry is used** (`icons/__tests__/registryUsage.test.js` parses src and fails on an entry no icon position references), so no unused tree ships. **Media controls and dots are SOLID** (`registry.js::SOLID_ICON_NAMES`: play, pause, step, stepBack, stop, skipForward, dot, liveRecording): `<Icon>` and `appendSvgIcon` fill them with the stroke colour, so the ControlPanel, the run bar and Vormachen cannot disagree; the Bewegung kind icon `record` stays an outline, and „Aufnahme läuft" uses `liveRecording`. React draws `<Icon name>` (decorative `aria-hidden`; a `title` makes it a named image; an unknown name throws outside production), so an icon-only button needs its own German `aria-label`. Blockly and CodeMirror SVG use `svg.js::appendSvgIcon`/`iconNodes` (a Blockly image data-URI `svg.js::iconMarkup`, e.g. the destination block's drive chip), which read an icon's shapes off the element react-icons returns: no render and no `react-dom/server` in any chunk. Toasts take `toast.js::toastIcon(kind)`, drawn in the toast's own text colour, and the one Toaster's `success`/`error` defaults are those icons (`src/toasterOptions.js`), so a bare `toast.success`/`toast.error` matches. **Fenced both ways**: `components/icons/__tests__/noIconGlyphs.test.js` tokenises every non-test src file with espree (a pinned devDependency) and walks `src/**/*.json` and `public/tutorials/*.json`, failing on the icon ranges (incl. U+2460–24FF, U+27C0–27FF and U+2800–28FF) after decoding escapes and HTML entities (typography stays: ← ↑ → ↓ ↔, „×" inside text, … · — „"), on a glyph built by `String.fromCodePoint`/`fromCharCode` from a literal, in the page HTML and in a `src/**/*.css` `content:` value; it allows an inline `<svg>` (JSX, markup or a data-URI) only in the files `INLINE_SVG_ALLOWED` names with a reason (charts, illustrations, the brand mark, Blockly images), refuses a CSS-drawn spinner (a bordered `animate-spin` element that is not `<Icon name="loading">`), and pins `react-icons` to exactly `registry.js` (/lu) and `custom.js` (/lib); `robotis_ai_setup/tests/test_no_icon_glyphs.py` checks every non-docstring Python string (and `chr(<literal>)`) of the shipped roots, the arm container's `docker/open_manipulator` scripts included. ControlPanel's liveness indicator turns the loader icon one step per `/task/status` message; never make it a free-running spin, which would claim a liveness nobody measured. A breakpoint's Protokoll line is plain text (`Haltepunkt erreicht: …`); `RunControls.jsx::BREAKPOINT_LOG_PREFIX` draws the pause icon in front of it and is lockstep-tested against the two server f-strings (`tests/test_breakpoint_log_prefix_lockstep.py`).
- **A code document has its own autosave hook, and that is not duplication.** `useAutosave` is keyed on the Blockly workspace, and a code workflow hands the page `onWorkspaceReady(null)`, so every path was a no-op for a Python/Java student. `code/useCodeAutosave.js` owns the code shape in its OWN IndexedDB bucket, which exists only while the open document IS code (two buckets under one namespace would both answer on mount and pull a student back into an old Python program). It inherits the Blockly draft's limitation: a draft is offered back only when NO cloud workflow is selected, so after a reload it returns as an UNSAVED document and saving creates a new workflow. The draft carries the document's Ziele (`destinations`); an older draft without them restores with an empty store.
- **Self-update**: the bundle bakes `REACT_APP_BUILD_ID`; `useVersionCheck` polls `/version.json` and reloads on a new buildId (60 s sessionStorage loop guard; `dev` skips). `useMeProfile` is the single `/me` owner (401/403→signOut, 404→German, 5xx→backoff); the account's `hf_username` is set by the token save, there is no auto-link from the ROS whoami any more. `supabaseClient.js` re-pushes the JWT to Realtime (`realtime.setAuth`) at boot and on every auth event (supabase-js does not on `INITIAL_SESSION`); `useSupabaseTrainings` layers a 30 s reconciliation poll + a 6 s active-job poll regardless of channel status.

## Windows GUI + installer (robotis_ai_setup)

- Everything the GUI writes lives in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\EduBotics\` (`.env`, `.last_image_pull.json`, `phone-cert/`) **except the diagnostics sink, the machine-wide leaf `%ProgramData%\EduBotics\logs\`**, resolved by `gui/app/constants.py::diagnostics_dir()` and shared by `device_manager._diagnostics_log_path()`, `gui_app._edubotics_diag_dir()` and six installer `.ps1` (bind_devices, configure_usbipd, install_prerequisites, migrate_from_docker_desktop, preflight_system, verify_system). It holds `install_diagnostics.log` plus the elevated transcripts. **The leaf is load-bearing**: those six `.ps1` grant `Users:Modify, ObjectInherit, NoPropagateInherit` on it, and granting it on the parent would let a standard user pre-create `…\EduBotics\wsl` and own `ext4.vhdx`. `import_edubotics_wsl.ps1` refuses a pre-existing `-InstallRoot` that is a reparse point. The resolver falls back to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\EduBotics` then `~\EduBotics` on unwritability (a real create-probe).
- Ports: web 80, camera-ingest 5557, Roboter-Studio control 8769 (127.0.0.1), phone HTTPS 8444. web_video 8080 + rosbridge 9090 are container-internal on Windows and loopback-only on the Pi.
- **Multi-line WSL scripts go through `wsl_bridge.run()`** (stdin bytes, CRLF-normalized). `wsl --list --quiet` output is BOM-less UTF-16LE; every consumer strips NULs before matching (PS scripts, `wsl_bridge`, the `.iss` Pascal `IsDistroRegistered`). **"Is the distro registered?" has three answers (registered / absent / unresponsive) with one implementation per language**: `gui/app/wsl_bridge.py::distro_registration` (+ the pure `resolve_distro_registration`; `docker_manager.distro_registration` delegates, `is_distro_registered` is its boolean) and the dot-sourced `installer/scripts/wsl_distro_state.ps1::Get-EduBoticsDistroRegistration`. A listing that answered is authoritative; when `wsl --list` fails, the `WSL_E_DEFAULT_DISTRO_NOT_FOUND` token means absent, otherwise `HKCU\...\Lxss\*\DistributionName` breaks the tie (a missing Lxss key is absent; a store naming the distro or an unreadable store is unresponsive). **Unresponsive is never reported as missing, never routed into finalize, never imported over and never reaches `wsl --unregister`**: `_run_prerequisite_checks_body` re-asks (`attempts=3`) and then stops with a German restart hint; the scan diagnosis sets `wsl_unresponsive`, not `wsl_distro_missing`. The twins are compared over one input table by `tests/test_installer_pwsh_executed.py::DistroRegistrationExecutedTest`.
- **A student window that ends on its own clears the robot's Hugging Face token.** `webview_window._watch_subprocess` reports a child that exited WITHOUT `destroy_all` having marked that Popen (a per-Popen attribute set before the close is requested, never the global `_deliberate_stop`, which the next spawn clears) to the hook registered with `set_exit_callback`, with the spawn's generation; `gui_app.py::_on_student_window_closed` forwards it on the watcher thread (never Tk) to `docker_manager.clear_robot_hf_token_after_window_close`.
