Deliberate ends are not reported: every `destroy_all` caller (`_on_close`, `_stop_environment._do_stop`, `_run_prerequisite_checks_body`, `_scan_arms._do_scan`, `_launch_installer_and_exit`) is followed by a container stop or a process exit that drops the tmpfs, and a new caller that does neither must clear explicitly. `clear_robot_hf_token` asks the NODE and never touches the file: `_HF_CLEAR_SCRIPT` (fixed text, no token in it) goes through `wsl_bridge.run` and, after a read-only `test -s` of `$HF_TOKEN_PATH`, calls `/register_hf_user` with an empty token (the wire `robotChannel.js` uses), so the node's own refusal while recording or transferring, its namespace-cache invalidation and its state publish all apply and a running upload keeps its token; deleting the file from outside makes `upload_large_folder` retry a failing call until the stall watchdog ends it. It reads only the stdout sentinel `EDUBOTICS_HF_CLEAR=` (`cleared` / `empty` / `no_container` / `refused` / `unreachable`), maps every failure to `unreachable`, never raises and never logs command output. Refused and unreachable retry (`HF_CLEAR_RETRY_DELAYS_S`, five attempts, about 8.5 min; since 2026-10-04 the node itself also remembers a clear it refused while busy and applies it once idle, so a refusal beyond the budget no longer leaves the token behind while the container runs), single-flight under `_hf_clear_lock`; `begin_session()` (every spawn, a refused spawn, `_webview_fallback`; not the live-child short-circuit) supersedes every queued retry and the loop asks `is_current_generation` after each sleep and right before an attempt, so a newer session always wins and a superseded clear logs nothing. Success is silent; an exhausted budget logs ONE German line (`HF_CLEAR_REFUSED_DE` / `HF_CLEAR_UNREACHABLE_DE`). Not covered: the system-browser fallback (no process to watch), a crashed or killed GUI (an orphaned child; the next start's `ensure_environment_stopped` drops the tmpfs), the Pi, and a push of the closing window itself still in flight at the close (`docs/KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). Fenced by `tests/test_gui_robot_token_clear.py`.
- **usbipd**: `Not shared` devices need an elevated `bind` first; `configure_usbipd.ps1`/`bind_devices.ps1` each try three `policy add` syntaxes across usbipd 5.x drift. Arms are OpenRB-150 boards: VID `2F5D`, PIDs `0103`/`2202`. `fast_rehydrate_arms(require_leader=True)` trusts saved leader/follower ports and returns `(None, None)` on any mismatch; the GUI then only prompts to rescan. On an `omx_follower` rig `_try_rehydrate_arms` passes `require_leader=False`. Readiness gates go through `_hardware_ready(profile)`, arm-family-aware.
- **Installer PS 5.1 traps** (CI-guarded): installer `.ps1` use `$ErrorActionPreference='Continue'` + `$LASTEXITCODE` (`configure_wsl.ps1` is the one sanctioned EAP=Stop file; it makes zero native calls); under EAP=Stop a native command writing stderr becomes a terminating `NativeCommandError` even on success (`ci.yml::powershell-native-stderr`). Shipped `.ps1` need a UTF-8 BOM; `.gitattributes` pins `*.ps1` to CRLF + UTF-8.
- **Rootfs upgrade gate protects student Docker volumes**: `wsl_rootfs/ROOTFS_VERSION` is baked into the rootfs as `/etc/edubotics-rootfs-version`; both `.iss::ShouldImportDistro` (German consent MsgBox) and `import_edubotics_wsl.ps1` skip the destructive `wsl --unregister` + re-import when versions match. Both fail OPEN on an unreadable stamp (`_rootfs_rebuild_required()`, `RootfsReimportNeeded()` block only on a positive mismatch). **The destructive re-import needs positive proof, never a failed read**: the existing distro's stamp is read by one command inside it that prints `EDUBOTICS_VM_UP` first and then the stamp or `EDUBOTICS_STAMP_ABSENT`. Only „VM up + absent" or „VM up + a different stamp" may lead to exit 12, the consent box or `wsl --unregister`; any other outcome is `NoProof`: `import_edubotics_wsl.ps1::Get-ExistingDistroStamp` exits 11 before the unregister, and `.iss::ProbeDistroStamp` (same sentinels, pinned equal by `OneRebootPredicateTest::test_the_installer_and_import_probe_the_stamp_with_the_same_proof`) makes `ShouldImportDistro` show no box. Never go back to classifying the failed read. Accepted price: a distro whose `ext4.vhdx` is truly gone is no longer rebuilt automatically. **Bump `ROOTFS_VERSION` whenever anything under `wsl_rootfs/` changes except `*.md`** (`ci.yml::rootfs-version-guard`; a bump forces a destructive fleet-wide re-import). Uninstall's distro wipe is consent-gated, default Nein; `[InstallDelete]` wipes `{app}\gui|scripts|wsl_rootfs` on every upgrade. `import_edubotics_wsl.ps1` hard-fails on a bad rootfs SHA256 sidecar and on <20 GB free before `wsl --import`.
- **`.reboot_required` means „deferred setup work is not finished", and its existence is only an entry router** (`gui_app.py::_reboot_required_pending`, `.iss::IsRebootRequired`), never a verdict. `install_prerequisites.ps1` keeps this run's reboot reason (`$needsReboot`) apart from a flag `-PreserveExistingRebootFlag` kept; finalize takes custody of the flag across its child (restoring content and original mtime only when `Test-RebootOutstanding` proves the reboot owed) and after that child does not exit 10 on the flag's existence; the verdict gate decides.
- **The reboot / virtualization verdict lives in exactly one place: `installer/scripts/virtualization_ready.ps1`**, dot-sourced (Test-Path-guarded) by `finalize_install.ps1`, `verify_system.ps1` and `preflight_system.ps1`. `Get-RebootState` is the only reader of the flag, the feature store and CIM. Two pure policies compose three proofs each spelled once (`Test-DdUninstallOutstanding`, `Test-FeatureEnablePending`, `Test-NoBootSinceFlag`; `OneRebootPredicateTest::test_neither_policy_re_spells_a_proof`): `Test-RebootOutstanding` (proof-only, `$false` on every unreadable state) and `Get-VirtualizationVerdict`, whose rung order is load-bearing: (1) dd-uninstall + no boot since → `RebootRequired`, (2) a feature `EnablePending` → `RebootRequired`, (3) `HypervisorPresent -eq $true` → `Ready` (ground truth above the Fast-Startup-unreliable boot-time inference), (4) no boot since the flag → `RebootRequired`, (5) `VirtualizationFirmwareEnabled -eq $false` → `VirtualizationDisabled`, (6) `Unknown`. **Only the `RebootRequired` rungs stop an import, and each asks for one restart per reason**: finalize writes `{app}\scripts\.restart_requested` (`Format-RestartRequest`: UTC boot ticks + the rung from `Get-RebootRequiredReason`) when it exits 10, and `Test-RestartDidNotHelp` turns the same rung after a real restart (`LastBootUpTime` moved by more than 60 s; Fast Startup's shutdown does not move it) into exit 11 with `$RESTART_DID_NOT_HELP_*`. A PC that reverts on restart (PC-Wächter, Deep Freeze, UWF) reverts the record too, so that loop is not bounded (`docs/KNOWN-ISSUES.md`). `VirtualizationDisabled` and `Unknown` proceed. Any new „is a reboot owed" decision that does not ask this file is the 2026-09-07 defect again.
- **Exit 11 comes from wsl's own report (or a restart that did not help), and its words come from proof.** `import_edubotics_wsl.ps1` captures `wsl --import` and classifies it with `Get-WslFailureClass` / `Get-WslFailureCode` by wsl's STAGE and CODE, never the localized message (`hypervisor` for the two HCS codes or a `CreateVm` stage; `disk` for a `MountVhd`/`AttachDisk`/`MountDisk` stage or a full-disk code; else `""`). A `hypervisor` import failure exits 11; a `disk` or unclassified one keeps exit 1. import hands code and class to finalize in `$global:EDUBOTICS_WSL_FAILURE_CODE` / `_CLASS`. finalize's `Fail-WithHypervisorRemedy` picks `Get-HypervisorRemedyKind`'s words: Disk on the disk class; Firmware only when CIM reads both `HypervisorPresent` and `VirtualizationFirmwareEnabled` genuinely `$false`; Feature on `HCS_E_HYPERV_NOT_INSTALLED`; Service on any other hypervisor class; Unclassified otherwise, appending „(Fehlercode: X)". Each kind's German problem + next step is declared once (`$VIRT_<KIND>_PROBLEM_DE`/`_NEXTSTEP_DE`) and reaches the GUI in the FAILED marker's two lines (`gui_app.py::_read_failed_marker`); the GUI's `VIRT_SERVICE_*`, `VIRT_FEATURE_*`, `VIRT_DISK_*`, `VIRT_UNCLASSIFIED_*` are pinned byte-equal (`test_the_gui_remedies_are_finalizes_words`), and the classifier's Python twin `wsl_bridge.classify_wsl_failure` is compared row by row (`ClassifierExecutedTest`). One dialog title for every exit-11 remedy: `VM_START_DIALOG_TITLE_DE`.
- **A registered distro that does not start is diagnosed by the GUI** (finalize never runs for it): `_run_prerequisite_checks_body` starts the distro with `docker_manager.probe_distro_start` → `wsl_bridge.probe_distro_start` (`wsl -d EduBotics --exec /bin/echo EDUBOTICS_VM_UP`; only the sentinel is proof). When wsl named a class, `_report_distro_cannot_start` shows the remedy at once; an unnamed miss still gets `wait_for_docker` and a second probe. The remedy (`_distro_start_remedy`, the kind rule minus Firmware) is kept as `_last_setup_outcome="virt"`. That early return sits above `ensure_environment_stopped()`, correctly (dockerd never answered). Every restart instruction says „Neu starten, nicht Herunterfahren". **These scripts are executed, not only grepped**: `tests/test_installer_pwsh_executed.py` runs the verdict, the classifier, the registration resolver, `install_prerequisites.ps1`, preflight, and finalize → import end to end with a fake `wsl` under `pwsh` (skips locally without `pwsh`/`$EDUBOTICS_PWSH`; under GitHub Actions a missing `pwsh` fails).
- `pull_images.ps1` retries GHCR 3× before Hub and prunes superseded tags by enumeration. `finalize_install.ps1` is the elevated post-reboot/GUI-invoked one-button repair, idempotent and state-gated (registration → `Wait-DockerReady` → `Test-ImagesPresent`; the one meaningful child code is import's 12), every failure via `Fail-WithNextAction`. **Its exit code is the contract with the GUI**: `0` done / `10` reboot still required / `11` no usable hypervisor / `12` rootfs rebuild needs consent / `1` failed, the first four mirrored as `gui_app.py::FINALIZE_EXIT_DONE`/`_REBOOT`/`_VIRT`/`_CONSENT` (there is no `FINALIZE_EXIT_FAILED`). `11` is distinct from `10` because a restart is not (or no longer) the fix. A WSL that does not answer is `1`, never `11`. `wsl_docker_ready.ps1::Wait-DockerReady` (dot-sourced; caller keeps EAP=Continue) is the sole implementation of dockerd readiness. Every dot-source is `Test-Path`-guarded (Controlled Folder Access can truncate `{app}\scripts`). `install_prerequisites.ps1` does not gate on Windows edition (build ≥ 22000 only). The `.iss` launches the GUI via `explorer.exe` (+ `ChangesEnvironment=yes`) so it inherits the post-install PATH; `CurStepChanged(ssInstall)` runs `docker compose down` before new files land.
- **The finalize marker is a three-shape, two-language contract, and its encoding is load-bearing.** `%ProgramData%\EduBotics\logs\edubotics_finalize.marker` disambiguates one cell: exit 0 while the un-elevated GUI still cannot see the distro (WSL2 registers distros per Windows user, and the installer is `PrivilegesRequired=admin`, so another admin's import lands in that admin's hive). `finalize_install.ps1` writes all three shapes with `-Encoding UTF8`, carrying `user=<name>` on `started …` and the terminal `SUCCESS <iso> user=… distro=…`, none on `FAILED <iso>\n<problem>\n<next step>`; `gui_app.py::_prompt_finalize_install::_marker_user` reads back as `utf-8-sig` and treats any U+FFFD in the name as unparseable; `preflight_system.ps1` check 5 parses the same field. `%USERNAME%` may contain spaces, so the value runs to end-of-line except in the SUCCESS shape, cut at `" distro="`. Names compare casefolded. `test_gui_install_lifecycle.py::PreflightAccountScopeTest` compares path, U+FFFD rule, encodings and delimiter; the casefold comparison and the `user=` key are still uncompared (`docs/KNOWN-ISSUES.md`).
- **A Schritt's colour is derived from its status TEXT** (`gui/app/theme.py::step_style_for`, bound by `trace_add` so every writer is covered): „gefunden…" and „wiederhergestellt…" read as done, „nicht gefunden"/„…fehlgeschlagen…" as an error, anything else as pending (the Hugging Face token has no Schritt in the GUI any more).
- Camera preview renders base64-PNG via `cv2.imencode` into `tk.PhotoImage` (Tk 8.6's binary `-data` is not NUL-safe); previews stop before the capture bridge claims devices, before rescans, and on close. `win_camera.camera_privacy_blocked()` surfaces the Windows camera-consent cause.

## Cloud stack (cloud_training_api + modal_training + supabase + jetson_agent)

- **Rate limiter** (in-process, `--workers 1`; `services/rate_limiter.py` + `main.py::_RATE_LIMIT_RULES`): rules sorted longest-prefix-first; `/jetson/`, `/trainings/`, `/workflows` are per-user keyed (`jwt:<sha256(token)[:16]>`, a hashed unverified token so a forger cannot starve a victim); everything else per-IP (leftmost X-Forwarded-For; safe behind Railway only). LRU cap 8192 keys/bucket. Method-pinned per prefix: `POST /workflows` 10/min (create, clone, restore, trajectory upload) and `PATCH /workflows` 30/min (saves + recording renames; autosave is local). `/me/hf-token` is per-user keyed too (a classroom shares one NAT IP) with method-pinned rules: GET 60/min, PUT 6/min, DELETE 10/min, `POST /reveal` 20/min, `POST /verify` 6/min; `GET /me/group-members` 30/min, per-user keyed for the same NAT reason (`test_workflow_submissions.py::TestRateLimitRulesUnchanged` pins the whole table in order). `BodySizeLimitMiddleware` rejects >384 KB on workflow/template writes before Pydantic materializes anything, and `PUT /me/hf-token` likewise (the middleware's 384 KB cap; the token itself is bounded by its 256-character shape, checked by hand).
- **The `/me/hf-token` routes** (`routes/hf_token.py`, `services/hf_credentials.py`): student-only, plain `def` handlers, `Cache-Control: no-store`, no user id in any signature (the row is always the JWT-verified caller's own, so there is no IDOR to assert). Token-semantics errors are **422, never 401/403** (the SPA signs out on those from `/me`), Hugging Face unreachable is 502, a missing key is 503, nothing stored is 404. `PUT` takes its body as an untyped `Body(default=None)` (FastAPI's default 422 echoes the offending `input`, i.e. the token, for any typed model and for a non-object body): the shape is checked by hand with `fullmatch`, `verify` makes its write CONDITIONAL in the database (045: `store_user_hf_credential(..., p_expected_fp)` locks the credential row FOR UPDATE and raises P0045 unless it still holds the fingerprint verify decrypted; the route maps it to 409), so it cannot write back a token removed or replaced while the Hub was asked (a Python re-read left that gap open), and no handler logs or raises a token, fingerprint or ciphertext (only user id + event, exception classes). The plaintext is returned only by `POST /reveal`, which also checks the decrypted token against the stored fingerprint; `POST /verify` re-asks the Hub and re-encrypts under the CURRENT key (how a rotated key is adopted, token by token); `DELETE` is idempotent, works without a key and keeps the proven `users.hf_username`. `EDUBOTICS_HF_TOKEN_KEY` (standard base64 of exactly 32 bytes) is a REQUIRED secret since 2026-10-04: absent or empty stops the boot in `main.py::_validate_required_secrets`, malformed in `_validate_hf_token_key`, and every place that imports `app.main` without Railway variables (CI, the pre-deploy schema probe) sets a dummy key (32 zero bytes); the routes keep their 503 branch; rotate by moving the old key to `EDUBOTICS_HF_TOKEN_KEY_PREVIOUS` (a token whose key is gone reads `usable:false` and the student saves it again). `cryptography` is imported inside the functions that need it, so the boot import probe and the stub-based route tests never need it, but `requirements.txt` pins it and `ci.yml::python-tests` must install it.
- **`GET /me/group-members` (Daten 2.0) answers the CALLER's current workgroup and nothing else** (`routes/me.py::read_my_group_members`, any role): the workgroup comes from the JWT-resolved profile, never from the request (the handler takes no other parameter, so a `?workgroup_id=` is dropped), so there is no id to tamper with (Rule §4); each member is exactly `{full_name, hf_username, is_me}` — no login `username`, no user id (a classmate's login is not the page's business); no workgroup (every teacher, a student alone) → the caller only, without a query; a database error → 500 „Gruppenmitglieder konnten nicht geladen werden.". It scopes the Daten tab's library (UI scoping, not a security boundary): the SPA asks once per signed-in user (`features/editDataset/hooks/useGroupNamespaces.js`, cached by user id) and a failure lists the student's own account alone. No migration; the schema probe is unchanged. `app/tests/test_me_group_members.py`.
- **Teacher-facing sub-surfaces, IDOR-gated like everything else**: `progress_entries` (three mutually-exclusive scopes enforced by DB CHECK + partial unique indexes), mid-training checkpoint listing (`HfApi.list_repo_files`, `_CKPT_CACHE_TTL_S` 30), per-student `inference_runs` (self-write keyed to the JWT, pruned to newest 50).
- **A student's Roboter-Studio work is four teacher reads, and none may key on `workflows.classroom_id`** (decision A3): `GET /teacher/students/{sid}/workflows` + `/{wid}`, `GET /teacher/students/{sid}/submissions` + `/{subid}`, each behind `_assert_student_owned` FIRST and then scoped on the ROW's own student column (`workflows.owner_user_id` / `workflow_submissions.student_user_id`). A client sets `classroom_id` at create with no membership check, so keying on it would surface one student's row under another teacher's classroom; a source-level test asserts the four bodies contain no `"classroom_id"`. The student side is `POST /workflows/{id}/submit` (under the existing `POST /workflows` 10/min rule) + `GET /workflows/{id}/submissions`. React: `components/teacher/StudentProgramsDrawer.js`, opened from `StudentRow`, read-only, no rosbridge, no browser-storage key, every failure one of its OWN German sentences chosen by HTTP status (never the thrown `Error.message`).
- **`update_workflow_code` is OWNER-only while `update_workflow_blockly` admits the classroom-template rule** (a teacher editing a code TEMPLATE goes through the owner rule because the teacher owns that row). Verified by reading, not by a test with real roles. Since 041 it takes a fifth argument, `p_blockly_json JSONB DEFAULT NULL`, written in the SAME UPDATE as the files (`COALESCE`, so one snapshot per save and an older API's four named arguments still resolve); the route sends it for a code row, and the boot probe calls the five-argument shape.
- **CORS `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` is boot-validated** (no `*` with credentials, no wildcards, absolute http(s) URLs).
- **Modal client rules** (`services/modal_client.py`): cancel/status use the blocking API in `asyncio.to_thread` (never `.aio()` on a `from_id` handle); `get_job_status` must inspect the returned dict's `status` (the worker returns `{"status":"failed"}` with exit 0); `cancel` keeps `terminate_containers=True`. `modal>=1.4,<2` pin; review before any 2.x bump.
- **Worker (`training_handler.py`)**: dataset preflight pinned to `revision=EXPECTED_CODEBASE_VERSION` (the `v3.0` tag; missing → German refusal), asserting layout completeness at that revision (≥1 `data/*.parquet`, `meta/episodes/*.parquet`, per-camera `videos/<key>/*.mp4`, `total_episodes ≥ 1`), then spawns `lerobot_train`, reports progress via the scoped RPC, self-terminates on P0001, mirrors intermediate checkpoints, and uploads full stdout as `training_log.txt` on every terminal path (028). The final upload stamps `edubotics_model_meta.json` next to `config.json`. The worker never writes `cancel_requested`. `_pretrained_load_failed` greps stdout for `_PRETRAINED_LOAD_FAILURE_MARKERS` (LeRobot-0.5.1-specific; fragile across bumps).
- **Migrations**: latest is **046** (`close_direct_student_writes`: `anon` and `authenticated` lose every write privilege and their own-row write policies on `trainings`, `datasets`, `workflows` and `tutorial_progress`, Rule §4; rollback re-grants and re-creates the policies as 024 defined them, re-opening the free-GPU-time hole), then **045** (`hf_credential_expected_fp`: `store_user_hf_credential` gains `p_expected_fp TEXT DEFAULT NULL`, compared under the credential row lock (P0045); the six-argument overload is dropped so exactly one exists, and an older API's six named arguments still resolve; rollback restores 042's writer verbatim, cloud API first), then **044** (`users_protected_columns`: the `public.users` column guard of Rule §4 plus the P0044 hf_username lock; rollback re-opens the hole), then **043** (`register_dataset_proven_anchor`: the proven account name is a second HF-author anchor; same signature; rollback restores 026 verbatim), then **042** (`user_hf_credentials`: a student's own Hugging Face token, AES-256-GCM encrypted by the cloud API; a service-role-only table plus the `store_user_hf_credential` writer that also sets `users.hf_username`; the rollback drops both and LOSES the stored tokens, so roll the cloud API back first; 042's header claim that the users-row lock stops a concurrent PATCH /me was wrong, PATCH /me never took it; 044 closed that), then **041** (`code_destinations`: `update_workflow_code` gains `p_blockly_json`, and the CHECK `workflows_code_blockly_json_destinations_only` allows a code row's `blockly_json` only its Ziele; rollback restores 040's four-argument body verbatim and keeps the data), then **040** (`code_programs`: `workflows.code_files` + `code_language`, the same pair on `workflow_versions` so the snapshot trigger versions a code document too, and the `workflow_submissions` table behind „Abgeben"; every column is DEFAULTED, which keeps an old API working against the new schema), then **039** (`trajectory_robot_profile_edu1`, widening 035's CHECK to `omx_f|edu6_studio|edu1_studio`), then **038** (`trainings_user_fk_cascade`). **037 is deliberately skipped**, claimed by the authored „Eigene Objekte" pair in `docs/plans/eigene-objekte-sql/`, which moves into `supabase/migrations/` when that feature lands (`supabase db push` orders by the timestamp prefix, not the `NNN` label; 038's header records the gap). Load-bearing: 036 (`edu6_arm_records`, service-role only), 035, 034 (`workflow_trajectories`), 033 (`workflows.sim_scene`), 030 (`users.hf_username`). Rollbacks in `supabase/rollback/`; manual data ops in `supabase/data_ops/` (post-deploy, never chained). `supabase/rollback/README.md` is stale (documents only through 009). The hand-run behavioural assertions (`supabase/tests/NNN_*_assertions.sql`, never CI) run on a local stack built from a SCRATCH copy whose baseline has its one `RENAME COLUMN runpod_job_id` line guarded, because the shipped baseline does not replay on a fresh database (`docs/KNOWN-ISSUES.md`); each header states its PASS count and run steps, fenced by `tests/test_supabase_assertion_headers.py`.
- **Jetson agent**: exclusive one-student claim, owner-pinned `:9091` rosbridge proxy, `SESSION_VOLUMES` (`jetson_workspace`, `jetson_huggingface_cache`) wiped on release (works only because the compose declares explicit `name:`s); auto-pulls with the GUI's digest set-membership pre-check; `docker-compose.jetson.yml` is standalone with `EDUBOTICS_FOLLOWER_ONLY=1`. `rosbridge_proxy.py` locks the JWT alg to config, pins the issuer, matches the owner `sub`, and caches ownership only `OWNER_CACHE_TTL_S` 0.2 s (claim/release handoff race). **It has zero test coverage** (`jetson_agent/tests/` holds only `test_agent.py`).
- Local run/test toggles: `EDUBOTICS_SKIP_SCHEMA_CHECK=1`, `DATASET_SWEEP_DISABLED=1` (gates spawning the loop), `DATASET_SWEEP_ENABLED=0` (no-ops each tick), `JETSON_SWEEPER_DISABLED=1`, `TRAINING_CANCEL_SWEEPER_DISABLED=1`.
- Known accepted edge: `register_dataset_safe`'s "delete-all then re-anchor" (low; `docs/CLAUDE-CHANGELOG.md`). Gemma-license gating for the pi0 family is documented-only, not enforced.

## Common commands

Test counts are dated snapshots (2026-09-16, macOS host; the setup-suite line was re-measured 2026-10-03 on a Linux sandbox); what is durable is the SHAPE. A number that no longer matches is a stale doc, not a regression; only a non-zero failure count means something.

### Run tests

```bash
# GUI / installer / deps-free contract tests (2025 on 2026-10-03, ~76 s on a Linux sandbox without pwsh; cross-platform)
cd robotis_ai_setup && python -m unittest discover -s tests -v
# tests/ has no __init__.py: dotted paths FAIL; use discover. On that sandbox (no pwsh, numpy or cv2) it ends `OK (skipped=81)`,
# provided tkinter imports (without one the 5 test_gui_theme tests ERROR; that is the environment, not a regression). The
# 2026-09-16 macOS snapshot (1532 tests) ended `OK (skipped=66)` without pwsh (57 executed installer tests skip) and
# `OK (skipped=9)` with pwsh + cv2/numpy on PATH, not re-measured. Of those 9,
# 4 are whole Windows-only modules (test_device_manager, test_docker_manager, test_docker_manager_wsl,
# test_wsl_path_convert), 2 are Windows-only in test_usbipd_resolver, 3 are test_gui_layout's opt-in
# Tk-render class behind EDUBOTICS_GUI_RENDER_TESTS=1.

# Orange Pi agent (deps-free, ~35 s; also NO __init__.py)
cd robotis_ai_setup/pi_agent && python -m unittest discover -s tests -v   # 587

# Server tests: Roboter Studio workflow + IK + node + Daten 2.0 (2946 pass / 18 skip on 2026-10-06, Python 3.12; pytest, ~450 s).
# Needs numpy + opencv and, for the Daten modules, pyarrow, av and huggingface_hub (CI pins the server image's versions on
# Python 3.12: pyarrow==25.0.0 av==15.1.0 huggingface_hub==1.23.0). A Python without those three still collects and runs
# everything else: each dependent module skips through pytest.importorskip. The LeRobot-only tests skip outside the server
# image; CI runs them inside it (docker-publish.yml::smoke-test).
cd physical_ai_tools/physical_ai_server && python -m pytest test/ -q

# Cloud API tests (301; stubs fastapi/supabase via sys.modules)
cd robotis_ai_setup/cloud_training_api
SUPABASE_URL=http://ci.test SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=ci_test \
MODAL_TOKEN_ID=ci_test MODAL_TOKEN_SECRET=ci_test \
python -m unittest discover -s app/tests -v

# Jetson agent (30; this dir HAS __init__.py)
cd robotis_ai_setup/jetson_agent && python -m unittest discover -s tests -v

# React (vitest, 132 files / 2034 tests, ~16 s). Run in CI as ci.yml::react-tests, the WHOLE suite.
cd physical_ai_tools/physical_ai_manager && npm test

# `--ext .js,.jsx` is NOT optional: without it eslint lints only `.js` and every `.jsx` goes unread.
npx eslint --ext .js,.jsx --max-warnings 0 src/
```

### Lint / validate

```bash
# Shell (mirrors ci.yml::shell-lint, 8 scripts)
shellcheck -S error robotis_ai_setup/docker/build-images.sh \
                    robotis_ai_setup/docker/open_manipulator/entrypoint_omx.sh \
                    robotis_ai_setup/wsl_rootfs/build_rootfs.sh \
                    robotis_ai_setup/wsl_rootfs/start-dockerd.sh \
                    robotis_ai_setup/jetson_agent/setup.sh \
                    robotis_ai_setup/pi_agent/setup.sh \
                    robotis_ai_setup/pi_agent/systemd/edubotics-pi-firstboot.sh \
                    physical_ai_tools/physical_ai_manager/scripts/railway-deploy.sh

# Compose (CI writes a fake .env first; without one expect "variable is not set" noise)
docker compose -f robotis_ai_setup/docker/docker-compose.yml \
               -f robotis_ai_setup/docker/docker-compose.gpu.yml config

# Python compileall: SEVEN roots, exactly as CI has them (CI carries a per-root zero-file floor)
python -m compileall -q robotis_ai_setup/gui robotis_ai_setup/scripts \
  robotis_ai_setup/cloud_training_api robotis_ai_setup/modal_training \
  robotis_ai_setup/pi_agent \
  physical_ai_tools/physical_ai_server/physical_ai_server \
  robotis_ai_setup/docker/physical_ai_server/patches
```

### Build images

**Default path for the thin images: GitHub Actions.** Push to `main` with changes under the docker paths and `docker-publish.yml` builds both arches. Local builds are dev-only (never push from a workstation):

```bash
cd robotis_ai_setup/docker
SUPABASE_URL=... SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=... CLOUD_API_URL=... ./build-images.sh   # amd64
PLATFORM=arm64 ./build-images.sh                                             # Jetson repos, skips manager + runner
PLATFORM=opi   ./build-images.sh                                             # Orange Pi repos, incl. manager + runner
```

**The three self-built bases are the exception; a workstation push is their only path** (Rule §6):

```bash
BUILD_BASE_OPI=1   PLATFORM=opi   ./build-images.sh   # Dockerfile.arm64cpu → physical-ai-server-opi-base
BUILD_BASE_ARM64=1 PLATFORM=arm64 ./build-images.sh   # Dockerfile.arm64 → both Jetson bases
```

All three secrets are required even for a base-only build (every flavor builds the manager first, and `${VAR:?}` aborts before the base build). An image rebuild is required whenever you touch `docker/open_manipulator/`, `docker/physical_ai_server/`, the overlays, the entrypoint, the `physical_ai_server` package, `docker/code_runner/`, or `physical_ai_interfaces` msg/srv; CI does those on a push. Hot-deploys into a running container are ephemeral.

### Deploy

**Always via GitHub Actions** (Rule §6). Full release: bump the version (below), then `git tag vX.Y.Z && git push --tags` (fires `release.yml`). Per-surface pushes to `main` fire the path-filtered workflow alone. **Before every tag: prove the installer builds** (`release-installer.yml` via workflow_dispatch with `version_tag` set to the PREVIOUS release; it is the only `.iss` Pascal compile check in CI). For releases touching the upgrade path (installer scripts, `wsl_rootfs/`), install the artifact twice on a dev rig (first shows the German consent + re-imports; second logs „Import übersprungen").

**Bumping product VERSION = 4 in-tree sites in one commit**: `VERSION`, `installer/robotis_ai_setup.iss` `AppVersion`, `gui/app/constants.py` fallback literal, `pi_agent/constants.py` fallback literal (both readers resolve the `VERSION` file at runtime; the literals are the last resort, and a stale pi_agent literal that reads LOW re-offers the same update forever). `release.yml::version-preflight` hard-fails a tag whose name ≠ the file and asserts the fallback literals equal it (a source grep; keep each literal a plain `return "X.Y.Z"`). W6 then auto-flips the Railway GUI-version vars.

Manual emergency paths (document in a PR): `supabase db push` / `psql -f rollback/NNN_*.sql`; `modal deploy modal_app.py`; `railway up --service scintillating-empathy --environment production --path-as-root . --ci` from `cloud_training_api/`; `build-images.sh` from a clean Linux host.

### Bootstrap (run once)

```bash
cd robotis_ai_setup && python scripts/bootstrap_admin.py --username admin --full-name "Sven"
```

## CI guardrails: `ci.yml`, 25 validator jobs on every push/PR to `main`

- **python-tests** (Python 3.12, the version the code runner ships; installs `pyarrow==25.0.0 av==15.1.0 huggingface_hub==1.23.0`, the server image's versions, so the suites import the Daten engine, the sync model, the sidecar and the download worker; LeRobot stays image-only): compileall (7 roots, zero-file floor each) + unittest discover over four trees (`tests/`, `cloud_training_api/app/tests/`, `jetson_agent/tests/`, `pi_agent/tests/`) + the physical_ai_server pytest suite. `pi_agent` is the one most easily forgotten.
- **shell-lint**: shellcheck `-S error` on the 8 shipped shell scripts.
- **compose-validate**: `docker compose config` on the student+gpu, Jetson and Orange-Pi composes plus the shipped `pi_agent/docker/` twin; asserts `.s6-keep` exists and that the student and opi composes bind it and target `127.0.0.1` in the manager healthcheck.
- **pi-compose-twin-guard**: `cmp -s` the opi compose against the tarball twin (a drifted twin makes the agent renew the wrong file while its own `config -q` gate proves VALID, never CURRENT).
- **overlay-guard**: runs `fix_server_inference.py` against a synthetic upstream; non-zero on no-op AND the patch must actually apply.
- **modal-import-validate**: imports `modal_app.py` (not `training_handler.py`, container-only deps) + smoke-imports the cloud routes touching the Modal SDK.
- **teacher-web-build-validate** / **manager-build-validate** / **manager-opi-build-validate**: build the three React Dockerfiles with placeholders and assert each reached the bundle; the opi variant also checks the `version.json` buildId and the `/pi-mode.json` marker.
- **tutorials-validate**: tutorial JSON + `allowed_blocks` vs the server dispatch tables + `HAT_BLOCK_TYPES` + Blockly built-ins.
- **interfaces-validate**: every `.srv` has exactly one `---`; CMakeLists ↔ on-disk cross-check.
- **nginx-validate**: `nginx -t` on all three configs, each with its own substitution (`nginx.web.conf.template` after `envsubst $PORT`, `nginx.conf` verbatim, `nginx.opi.conf.template` after `envsubst $EDUBOTICS_ROS_NET_GATEWAY`).
- **base-version-guard**: a non-comment edit to `Dockerfile.arm64cpu` / `.arm64` must move the matching `PAS_BASE_IMAGE` tag in `build-images.sh`.
- **env-forwarding-guard**: every `EDUBOTICS_*` read by the entrypoint/start-dockerd/ingest node/driver/server package/overlay `.py`s must appear in a compose `environment:` list SOMEWHERE (a union over all composes; blind to a key on one target only or on the wrong service).
- **compose-env-parity**: `.github/scripts/compose_env_parity.py` compares `docker-compose.yml` against `docker-compose.opi.yml` service by service, gating KEY PRESENCE only. An `INTENTIONAL` allowlist carries a written reason per divergence (today exactly one: the opi manager's `EDUBOTICS_ROS_NET_GATEWAY`) and a stale entry is a hard error; the parser reads one shape (`- KEY=value`) and refuses every other; `REQUIRED_ENV_SERVICES` (`open_manipulator`, `physical_ai_server`) must parse to non-zero entries; service-set equality is enforced. Residual: a name forwarded only by the Jetson or gpu compose satisfies both guards.
- **powershell-encoding**: every shipped `.ps1` carries a UTF-8 BOM. **powershell-native-stderr**: no `.ps1` pairs file-level EAP=Stop with a stderr-capturing native call. **powershell-install-guards**: runs the four `ps_*` guards (`tests/test_ps_guard_scripts.py` pins them; three share `.github/scripts/_ps_lex.py`, `ps_temp_path_normalize_lint.py` does its own scan); all four refuse a zero-file scan.
- **german-strings-lint**, **rootfs-version-guard**, **enum-parity** (wire-int cross-check `.srv`/`.msg` ↔ `taskCommand.js`/`taskPhases.js` ↔ getattr fallbacks ↔ hardcoded test ints), **secret-scan** (gitleaks, pinned + sha256-verified, full history; `.gitleaksignore`), **react-lint** (`npx eslint --ext .js,.jsx --max-warnings 0 src/`), **react-tests** (`npm test`, the WHOLE suite; never narrow it to a subset), **actions-security** (zizmor, pinned, `--min-severity low`, baseline zero; the one accepted deviation, `release.yml secrets: inherit`, is documented in `.github/zizmor.yml`).

(`image_source_parity.sh` runs in `docker-publish.yml::smoke-test`, not ci.yml.)

### Deploy-workflow hardening invariants

- Health gates verify identity (commit sha / buildId), never a bare 200.
- **docker-publish**: native per-arch runners (amd64 `ubuntu-latest`, arm64 `ubuntu-24.04-arm`, no QEMU; `fail-fast: false`); `jlumbroso/free-disk-space` on amd64 + opi; `--no-cache --pull`; dirty-tree refusal; buildkit routes docker.io through `mirror.gcr.io`; smoke-test pulls the GHCR images and asserts arch, bundle env, amd64 **< 7 GB of uncompressed layer bytes** measured with `docker image history --human=false`, deliberately not `docker image inspect .Size` (which reads COMPRESSED under the containerd snapshotter and would make the gate inert); the opi ceiling stays 11 GB on `.Size`. Tag set: `:latest` (main), `:<sha>`, `:<short-sha>`, and on tags `:X.Y.Z` + `:X.Y`. GHCR retags fatal, Hub best-effort. smoke-test also runs `.github/scripts/record_fsm_smoke.py` inside the amd64 and opi server images against the real LeRobot 0.5.1 writer, after the step that asserts the pulled image is THIS run's build (FINISH in warm-up, FINISH < 1 s, skip, discard+finish, „Wiederholen" in the first episode, one normal episode, and since round 5 `slot_sampler_take` (a 10 s take decided by the slot sampler from synthetic timestamped histories with burnt-in counters, two cameras off 30 Hz, jitter and a 300 ms stall: exactly the planned frames, no lost slot, only honest repeats/skips), `frame_drop_rerecord` (the public `create(encoder_queue_maxsize=1)`, hevc, noise: drops seen through LeRobot's warning, two re-records, the third ends the session), `multitask_next_first_warmup`, `source_gap_redo` (two redos, the third kept), `official_discard_timing` (< 1.5 s) and, since round 6, `queued_finish_during_discard` (RERECORD, then FINISH from another thread while the discard holds the recorder lock: accepted at once, applied at the release, only the first episode on disk; `slot_sampler_take` now feeds the real encoder at wall-clock speed); ticks paced on a monotonic 30 Hz deadline, on-disk frames compared with the frames the writer was fed, timing preconditions read off the DataManager's own clocks), then `.github/scripts/daten_smoke.py` in the same two images (Daten 2.0, `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1`, one JSON line: a 2-camera dataset written by the shipped recorder writer in two sessions; delete / split / merge with the full check — LeRobot loads the output, every kept frame decodes pixel-equal at the train-time timestamps, statistics equal LeRobot's aggregate —; `verify` and its mutations, one clip per camera, the layout, unaligned and statistics refusals, the data-file roll-over, the three-way „Beide behalten", the upload's integrity gate and the hints), and the LeRobot-only server test files (`test_v3_surgery_compat_verify.py`, `test_v3_surgery_layout.py`, `test_dataset_card.py`, the only place CI runs them) against the shipped package in both images, where a skip fails the step; the image name goes through `env:`, never `${{ }}` inside `run:` (zizmor baseline).
- **Concurrency**: `ci.yml` cancels in-flight PR runs (never main); `docker-publish.yml` never cancels and runs two lanes, a per-tag release lane vs one shared `main` lane, keyed on `github.ref`, NOT `github.event_name` (inside a reusable workflow it reports the caller's event and would collapse both lanes). `release.yml` cancels on a newer tag; `supabase-migrate.yml` serializes on a project-scoped group. Timeouts: docker build 60 min, retag 45 min, installer 90 min.
- **Caller-permissions-superset rule**: each `release.yml` caller job must superset its callee's declared permissions, or GH `startup_failure`s the run.
- Every checkout: `persist-credentials: false` (44 of 44). All third-party actions SHA-pinned. Template injection defused via env indirection. Inno Setup pinned 6.7.0. `softprops/action-gh-release` needs the explicit `token:` under `workflow_call`.
- **Secrets consumed by CI** (10 + auto `GITHUB_TOKEN` + repo var `SUPABASE_BRANCHING_ENABLED`): `DOCKERHUB_USERNAME/TOKEN`, `RAILWAY_TOKEN`, `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN/DB_URL/PROJECT_REF/URL/SERVICE_ROLE_KEY/ANON_KEY`, `REACT_APP_CLOUD_API_URL`. `MODAL_TOKEN_ID/SECRET` are used by no workflow (operator's manual `modal deploy` only).
- Supabase PR-branch flow is gated behind both the dashboard Branching feature and `SUPABASE_BRANCHING_ENABLED=true`; unset → both jobs skip green.

## When to ask the user

Act autonomously on: reading files, editing code with low blast radius, running local tests/lints, building images locally, spawning sub-agents for research.

**Ask first** for:

- `git push` (never force-push to `main`)
- `wsl --unregister EduBotics` (destroys the VHDX → volumes `ai_workspace`, `huggingface_cache`, `edubotics_calib` gone)
- `docker compose down -v` or `docker volume rm` of `huggingface_cache` / `edubotics_calib`
- Force-push, `git reset --hard`, `git clean -fd`, `--no-verify`
- Modal `cancel` on a running training (charges credit)
- `supabase.auth.admin.delete_user` calls
- Rotating production secrets, editing CI/CD config
- Changing safety-critical paths (torque-disable, sync-verification tolerance, ownership assertions, Dynamixel current limits, the manual-mode arbiter/lock order, the motion-lock policy, collision gating, the edu6 torque-on guards, `LINK_RADIUS_M + ZONE_MARGIN_M`, the two HOME families)
- Renaming files other layers reference (overlay targets, ROS topics, env var names, RPC signatures)
- Touching `start_training_safe` / `get_remaining_credits` / `adjust_workgroup_credits` semantics (the workgroup credit pool is load-bearing)
- Reintroducing software-side inference safety guards (Rule §2)

## Spec-before-code for multi-layer features

Any feature touching more than one of the 10 layers gets a one-page dated spec+plan in `docs/plans/` BEFORE the first commit, with an explicit **"Non-goals (this round)"** section carrying trigger conditions. `docs/plans/` is gitignored and throwaway; delete the page once the feature lands. Durable invariants graduate into this file; dated narratives go to `docs/CLAUDE-CHANGELOG.md`; living status goes to `docs/KNOWN-ISSUES.md`.

## When in doubt

The single source of truth is **the code**. This file describes invariants at the time it was written; verify against `git log` and the current file when stakes are high, and fix this file in the same change when it disagrees.

When you find an obstacle, **find the root cause** instead of bypassing it (never `--no-verify`, never `@pytest.skip`, never short-circuit an `apply_overlay` sha256 assertion that is telling you upstream renamed something).
